# UI Pass and Add-Camera Flow Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Make the rtsp-warden web UI consistent, phone-friendly and credential-safe, rebuild the broken ONVIF page, and let an admin add (with a connection test and ONVIF URL fill), edit and delete cameras from the browser, with logins kept out of `config.yaml` and changes applied to the running process without a restart.

**Architecture:** Every route module renders through the one `Jinja2Templates` instance in `web/routes/_common.py`, which also provides `set_flash` (a one-shot `warden_flash` cookie that `ContextMiddleware` turns into `request.state.flash`) and `is_htmx`; htmx requests get HTML fragments and plain form posts get a 303 plus a flash. Camera add/edit/delete live in a new `web/routes/camera_edit.py` (plain `def` handlers, so blocking work runs in the threadpool) on top of offline-testable services: `probe.py` (ffprobe plus a one-frame snapshot), `onvif/soap.py` + `onvif/media.py` (WS-UsernameToken `GetStreamUri` over ports 80, 8080, 888, 2020), `web/services/camera_config.py` + `web/services/camera_edit.py` (raw-YAML append/patch/remove through `_locked_write_yaml`) and `web/env_file.py` (locked, mode 0600 `.env` upsert). The running process changes only through RW-0's `AppRuntime.request_add_camera` / `request_restart_camera` / `request_remove_camera` futures with bounded waits (20 s add, 25 s edit/delete), and `ffmpeg.redact_text` strips credentials from every ffmpeg/ffprobe error line before it is stored or shown. The ONVIF page becomes server-rendered forms with htmx fragments, and PTZ preset writes patch only that camera's raw `presets` key.

**Tech Stack:** Python (this branch keeps `requires-python = ">=3.10"`), FastAPI on Starlette 1.3.1, Jinja2 3.1.6, htmx 2.0.4, Alpine.js, Pico CSS 2, httpx 0.28.1 (`httpx.MockTransport` in tests), PyYAML, pydantic v2, ffprobe/ffmpeg through `subprocess.run`, pytest + pytest-asyncio (`asyncio_mode = "auto"`), ruff, uv.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-02-detection-and-automation-design.md`, Appendix A.2 "UI pass (bounded)", all four bullets (consistent layout, nav and forms plus the ONVIF page; live camera cards; the add-camera flow with Test connection, ONVIF URL fill, save + hot-add, edit and delete; the mobile-width check), which is item 2 of section 3 "Order of work". Where the planning rulings replace a spec sentence, the next section says so.

**Branch / worktree:** `feat/ui-pass`; WT = `/home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2`. RW-0 (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-02-rw0-prefork.md`) commits directly on `master` first: Task 0.1 (anchored `.gitignore` rules, the three never-tracked templates, `.worktrees` ignored) and Task 0.2 (per-camera lifecycle API on `AppRuntime`). Only after both RW-0 commits are on `master`: `git -C /home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0 worktree add .worktrees/rw-2 -b feat/ui-pass master`, then `uv sync --directory /home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2`, then a green baseline `uv run --directory /home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2 pytest -q`. This branch merges first; RW-3 (`feat/detection`, `.worktrees/rw-3`) rebases on it.

## Global Constraints

- Tooling: `uv` only (no pip, no new dependencies on this branch; RW-3's `uv.lock` wins at merge).
- Gate after every task: `uv run --directory /home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2 pytest -q` all green; `uv run --directory /home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2 ruff check src/ tests/` reports nothing but the baseline of exactly 10 E501 lines (`app.py` x3, `cli.py` x2, `proxy/mjpeg.py`, `recorder.py`, `retention.py`, `tests/test_frame_tap_wiring.py` x2; line numbers may move, the count never grows; a touched baseline line is fixed and the baseline shrinks); `uv run --directory /home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2 ruff format --check src/ tests/` clean. mypy is informational only.
- Python floor: this branch keeps `requires-python = ">=3.10"` and ruff `target-version = "py310"`; R2's `>=3.11` floor and `pyproject.toml` changes belong to RW-3, so RW-2 code must run on 3.10 (for example `datetime.timezone.utc`, not `datetime.UTC`).
- Secrets: never commit, print or log a credential (not in a test name, fixture, commit message or log line); test RTSP URLs use `rtsp://u:p@h/m`; `config.yaml` only ever holds `${CAM_<SLUG>_USER}` / `${CAM_<SLUG>_PASS}` references; values live in `<config dir>/.env` (mode 0600); in Task 12 only the owner types the real Foscam login.
- `config.yaml` stays authoritative; every write-back goes through `web/config_lock._locked_write_yaml` (via `web/services/camera_config.append_camera` / `patch_camera` / `remove_camera`); a write `OSError` becomes a flash or fragment naming the path, never a 500 (R9).
- Live segment recording stays `.ts`; never reintroduce MP4 live segmenting.
- No Raspberry Pi or GPIO work anywhere.
- `sub_url` stays optional everywhere (blank clears it; every consumer falls back to main); the owner's Foscam uses `main_url` only.
- Commit after every task with the message given in its commit step, ending with the trailer `Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>` (as decisions.md and every task write it); stage paths by name, never `git add -A`; never push without the owner's explicit OK.
- Hands-off for this track (RW-3 owns them; see "File ownership" for the full list): `detectors/`, `db/`, `migrations/`, `alerts/`/`actions/`, `recorder.py`, `frame_tap.py`, `clips.py`, `retention.py`, `retention_resolver.py`, `status_model.py`, `pyproject.toml`, `uv.lock`, `Dockerfile*`, `examples/`, `web/routes/{events,api,clips,recordings,alerts}.py`, `web/routes/cameras.py` from `cameras_detectors_partial` (master line 387) to the end, `web/services/{events,recordings,timeline,timeline_colors,preview}.py`, templates `events/*`, `alerts/*`, `recordings/*`, `clips/*`, `partials/event_row.html`, `partials/detector_list.html`, `partials/timeline.html`, `cameras/detection_classes.html`, `cameras/sensitivity.html`; and byte-for-byte unchanged: the detectors `<article>` of `cameras/detail.html` and the "Recent events" / "Recent recordings" blocks of `dashboard.html`.
- Worktree command rules: every command runs as `uv run --directory /home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2 ...` (or after `cd <WT> &&`), every git command as `git -C /home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2 ...`, and every file path is absolute under the worktree.
- Tests are offline: no camera, no ffmpeg/ffprobe process, no network; `subprocess.run` is patched where it is looked up at call time, HTTP goes through `httpx.MockTransport`, time is injected (`now`, `clock`); log assertions monkeypatch the module logger, never `caplog`.
- Status codes existing tests pin stay as they are: `POST /users/new` 200, token create/revoke 200, user delete/toggle-admin 303 for non-htmx posts, and `/login` contains no `hx-post`.
- No browser harness: markup is asserted through `TestClient`; one measured Browser-pane pass at 375 px (Task 11) and one owner-gated camera pass (Task 12).

## Decisions and deviations from the spec

Rulings from the planning decisions file that this plan relies on:

- **R1.** RW-0 lands on `master` before the fork; this plan only consumes `AppRuntime.request_add_camera` / `request_restart_camera` / `request_remove_camera` (thread-safe, return futures that the supervisor resolves on the main thread) and never edits the lifecycle code.
- **R2 (boundary).** The Python 3.11 floor, onnxruntime and every `pyproject.toml` / `uv.lock` change are RW-3's; this branch adds no dependency and stays 3.10-compatible.
- **R3.** Logins typed into the form are written to `config.yaml` only as `${CAM_<SLUG>_USER}` / `${CAM_<SLUG>_PASS}` (`<SLUG>` = name upper-cased, `-` to `_`); the percent-encoded values go to `<config dir>/.env` (mode 0600, locked upsert) and `os.environ`; `serve`, `doctor` and `status` read `<config dir>/.env`, then `./.env`, never overriding a set variable (Tasks 6, 7, 8, 10).
- **R5 (boundary).** The recordings UI is removed by RW-3; this plan only drops the always-empty "Recent recordings" table from the camera detail page (Task 3) and leaves the dashboard block untouched.
- **R7.** ONVIF page option A: server-rendered forms and htmx fragments, routes read form fields (no `request.json()`); `PTZPresetStore._persist` becomes a raw-YAML patch under the lock; `CameraConfig.onvif_port` (default 80) feeds `_derive_onvif_xaddr`; WS-UsernameToken is used only by the add-camera URL fill, PTZ and events keep HTTP Digest (Tasks 5, 6, 9).
- **R9.** Compose mounts `./config` read-write and the systemd unit lists `/etc/rtsp-warden` in `ReadWritePaths`; every config write-back route catches `OSError` and shows a message naming the path (Tasks 7, 8, 9, 10).
- **R10.** The name is read-only on edit (rename = delete + add); editable: `main_url`, `sub_url` (blank clears), login (blank keeps), `record.enabled`, `onvif_port`; proxy port auto-allocated (lowest free port >= 9001 not used by another camera) and shown read-only; delete keeps recordings, thumbnails, clips and events and says so; names match `^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,31}$`, are unique and are not `new`; `/cameras/{name}/settings` is a 303 to `/cameras/{name}/edit` (Tasks 3, 6, 7, 8).
- **R14 (boundary).** RW-3 renames the Alerts nav item to Actions and drops Recordings; Task 1's nav pattern (`aria-current` per item) is what RW-3 must follow.
- **R22.** The Foscam check (Task 12) is the last task, runs only after the owner says yes in chat and never gates the merge.

Spec sentences replaced because the code proved their premise false:

- A.2 bullet 1, "fix the broken htmx and Alpine bindings on the ONVIF page": the bindings cannot be fixed in place (htmx never processes the `:hx-post` attributes Alpine writes after load, and the routes call `request.json()` on form posts). Replaced by R7: the page is rebuilt as server-rendered forms with htmx fragments and no Alpine (Task 9).
- A.2 bullet 2, "failed with last ffmpeg error line": the raw stderr line echoes the RTSP URL with the password. Replaced by: the last error line after `ffmpeg.redact_text`, which also runs before the 300-character cut (Task 3).
- A.2 bullet 3, "save writes `config.yaml` and hot-adds the camera": on master the shipped Docker mount is `:ro` and the systemd unit has `ReadOnlyPaths=/etc/rtsp-warden` (R9, Task 10), the only existing write-back (`PTZPresetStore._persist`) dumped the env-expanded model, secrets included, into the file (R7, Task 9), and there was no per-camera runtime API to hot-add with (R1, RW-0). Replaced by: save writes `.env` first, then the raw YAML entry with env references, then hot-adds through the RW-0 queue, and says "takes effect when rtsp-warden restarts" when no runtime is attached (Task 7).
- A.2 bullet 3, "ONVIF (WS-UsernameToken, trying ports 80, 8080, 888, 2020)": scoped by R7 to the add-camera URL fill; PTZ and event subscriptions keep HTTP Digest and the global `onvif:` credentials.

Deviations reported by the task sections (from the skeleton or the spec text):

- Task 1: the flash cookie holds base64url of `{"m", "l"}` JSON, cut to 500 characters (plain JSON is not a legal cookie value and headers are latin-1), in a new module `web/flash.py`; it is consumed only by a full-page `200 text/html` GET, so htmx polls and redirect chains leave it pending, and `set_flash` is never called on an htmx fragment response. The footer reads `app_version | default(request.app.version)` because RW-3's events/recordings/clips modules still build their own templates; the page-header action slot is `header_actions` (RW-3's Actions page passes `actions`).
- Task 2: adds `is_htmx` to `_common.py`; the new-user and reset-password pages become plain forms; `partials/password_reveal.html` no longer puts the one-time secret inside a JavaScript string (an admin-typed `'` could inject script).
- Task 3: `redact_text` also masks `passwd=` / `pass=` and is greedy to the last `@` of a token (raw `@ / # ?` in a password stay covered); a status poll for a removed camera answers htmx with 286 (htmx stops polling) instead of a 404 it would retry forever; viewers no longer see admin-only controls; `cameras/settings.html` is deleted; the detail page keeps its own `#camera-actions` row rather than the page-header slot.
- Task 4: the snapshot argv adds `-vf scale='min(640,iw)':-2 -q:v 5` and both calls pass `stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, check=False` beyond the skeleton argv; `validate_rtsp_url` also rejects raw `/ ? # @` in userinfo and option-like input; `ProbeResult` fields default to None; ffprobe is not added to `cli._require_binaries`.
- Task 5: adds an unauthenticated `GetSystemDateAndTime` step so `wsu:Created` follows the camera clock, `OnvifAuthError(OnvifError)`, an HTTP Digest fallback, a `clock` parameter and the extra test file `tests/test_onvif_soap.py`; the Media XAddr keeps its path but takes the host AND port that answered; a bad host or port list raises `OnvifError`, never `ValueError`.
- Task 6: names are unique by `env_slug` (stricter than case-insensitive: `front-door` and `front_door` collide); the config-dir `.env` is read before `./.env`; `_parse_dotenv_value` unescapes `\"` and `\\`; adds `_load_dotenv_file`, `env_var_names`, `credential_env_values`, `url_with_env_credentials`, a module `_RMW_LOCK`, a rename refusal in `patch_camera` and the `_onvif_port_valid` validator; rewrites `tests/test_dotenv_parse.py` and `tests/test_cli_dotenv.py` (old tests kept).
- Task 7: a stream field holds a path or a credential-free URL, and a URL carrying `user:pass@` is rejected; `.env` is written before `config.yaml`; `cfg.cameras` is rebound copy-on-write under `_config_edit_lock`; a typed ONVIF port limits the lookup to that port; the htmx endpoints always answer 200 with the error inside the fragment; no config file gives a 503 form, an `OSError` a 303 to `/cameras/new` with an error flash.
- Task 8: adds the module `web/services/camera_edit.py` (not in the skeleton); delete is the one `async def` route and stops the ONVIF subscriber through the `onvif/events.py` registry (no helper in `routes/onvif.py` is needed); the config change lands before the runtime wait; a runtime future failing with `ValueError` ("not running") counts as no runtime; an ONVIF port change does not restart the ingest; the edit form hides Host, Test and ONVIF buttons (they need the plaintext password).
- Task 9: `PTZPresetStore(..., persist=...)` with a default `_config_file_persist` that calls `patch_camera`; module-level `_ptz_client` / `_event_client` factories for tests; preset delete becomes `POST /onvif/cameras/{name}/ptz/delete` with form field `preset_name`; `GET /onvif/cameras/{name}/ptz` becomes a 303; `warden.js` gains an `htmx:beforeSwap` listener that swaps 4xx `text/html` responses; WS-Discovery runs in `asyncio.to_thread`.
- Task 10: `examples/config.yaml` is NOT edited (RW-3 owns it, although the skeleton listed it); the task also edits `packaging/systemd/install.sh` (config dir mode 0770), `packaging/systemd/README.md` and `docker/README.md`, which neither track owns, and adds `tests/test_deploy_writable.py`.
- Task 11: the zones page opened the zone editor as a bare fragment (no CSS, Alpine or viewport tag); a normal request now gets `cameras/zones_editor_page.html` and htmx keeps the fragment; the editor placeholder becomes `.zone-placeholder` with an aspect-ratio binding; the zones, settings and health tables get `.table-wrap`.
- Task 12: runs `serve` on port 8098 from the git-ignored scratch folder `.worktrees/rw2-foscam-scratch`; a known risk is recorded ahead of time: `/status.json` (no login) returns the raw ffmpeg stderr tail through `cli._proc_status` (`src/rtsp_warden/cli.py:236-242`, not an RW-2 file), so a leak there becomes a defect for RW-3, not a fix inside Task 12.

## Task order and dependencies

Run the tasks strictly in numeric order: line numbers and test-count deltas in each task assume every earlier task is on the branch.

- RW-0 Tasks 0.1 and 0.2 -> on `master`, before the worktree exists.
- Task 1 (UI shell) -> worktree created after RW-0.
- Task 2 (users/tokens htmx) -> Task 1.
- Task 3 (camera cards, detail, `redact_text`) -> Task 1.
- Task 4 (`probe.py`) -> Task 3 (`redact_text`).
- Task 5 (ONVIF soap/media) -> worktree only.
- Task 6 (config services, `.env`, `ports.py`, `onvif_port`) -> worktree only.
- Task 7 (add-camera routes) -> RW-0 Task 0.2, Tasks 1, 3, 4, 5, 6.
- Task 8 (edit and delete) -> RW-0 Task 0.2, Tasks 1, 3, 4, 6, 7.
- Task 9 (ONVIF page) -> Tasks 1, 2, 6.
- Task 10 (writable deployments, docs) -> Task 6 (`doctor` reads the config-dir `.env`); documents Tasks 1 and 3-9.
- Task 11 (mobile width) -> Tasks 1, 2, 3, 7, 8, 9 (runs after 1-10).
- Task 12 (Foscam, manual) -> Tasks 1-11 and the owner's OK.

Spec coverage (Appendix A.2):

- Bullet 1, consistent layout, nav and form styling; ONVIF page bindings -> Tasks 1, 2, 3, 9 (checked at phone width in Task 11).
- Bullet 2, camera cards with live status (running, restarting with countdown, failed with last ffmpeg error) -> Task 3.
- Bullet 3, add-camera flow: form, Test connection (ffprobe with timeout; codec, resolution, fps, snapshot) -> Tasks 4, 7; ONVIF WS-UsernameToken URL fill over 80/8080/888/2020 with host rewrite -> Tasks 5, 7; save writes `config.yaml` and hot-adds -> Tasks 6, 7, 10; edit and delete from the detail page -> Task 8; live end-to-end check -> Task 12.
- Bullet 4, mobile-width check for every page -> Task 11 (live-camera glance in Task 12 Step 12).

## Review Focus

1. A password containing `/`, `#`, `?`, `@`, `%` (or `$`) is percent-encoded into `.env`, never reaches `config.yaml`, still expands to a URL with the right host, port, path and password, and never appears on a page, in an error or in a log: pinned by Task 6 `TestCredentialRoundTrip`, Task 7 `test_save_stores_a_percent_encoded_password` and `test_connection_test_percent_encodes_reserved_password_characters`, Task 8 `test_new_password_with_reserved_characters_goes_to_env_file_only`, Task 3 `test_redact_text`, Task 4 `test_percent_encoded_special_password_never_reaches_error` and Task 5 `test_signed_requests_carry_a_valid_digest_and_never_the_password`.
2. A valid name that equals an existing camera in another case (or differs only in `-` versus `_`) is rejected before anything is written: pinned by Task 6 `TestNames::test_duplicate_in_another_case_is_rejected` and `test_names_sharing_a_slug_are_rejected`, and Task 7 `test_save_rejects_an_existing_name_in_another_case` (422, `config.yaml` and `.env` untouched, runtime never called).
3. With no runtime attached (`app.state.runtime` is None) add, edit and delete still write `config.yaml` and `.env` and say the change takes effect on restart: pinned by Task 7 `test_save_without_runtime_writes_files_and_says_restart` and Task 8 `test_edit_without_runtime_saves_and_says_restart_needed` / `test_delete_without_runtime_still_updates_config`.
4. The ONVIF fill on a dead host costs one attempt per port at the 3 s timeout (4 x 3 s ceiling) and the message names every port tried: pinned by Task 5 `test_dead_host_costs_one_attempt_per_port_at_the_timeout` and Task 7 `test_onvif_fill_failure_names_the_ports_tried`.
5. Deleting a camera whose ffmpeg is wedged returns within the 25 s future timeout with an error flash while the `config.yaml` and `cfg.cameras` change has already landed: pinned by Task 8 `test_delete_with_wedged_ffmpeg_times_out_but_config_change_lands` (the add path's equivalent is Task 7 `test_save_does_not_wait_forever_for_the_runtime`).

### Task 1: UI shell: one templates instance, flash messages, nav, page header, CSS primitives

Goal: every RW-2 route module renders through the single `Jinja2Templates` instance in
`web/routes/_common.py`; routes can attach a one-shot flash message to a redirect and the next page
shows it once; the nav wraps on narrow screens and marks the current section; a reusable page header
partial and the CSS primitives (`.table-wrap`, `.status-badge`, `.flash-*`, `.small`, `.sr-only`,
`.field`, `a.button`, phone media rules) exist for the later tasks. No page content changes besides the
footer version, the nav, and a "You have been logged out." message on `/login`.

Design notes the engineer needs (all verified against Starlette 1.3.1 / Jinja2 3.1.6 / httpx 0.28.1 in a
scratch copy of the repo; the whole task was applied there and the full suite passed):

- **Flash cookie format.** `warden_flash` holds base64url (no `=` padding) of
  `json.dumps({"m": message, "l": level})`. Plain JSON is not a legal RFC 6265 cookie value (quotes,
  commas, spaces) and only round-trips through `SimpleCookie`'s octal quoting; a non-ASCII message
  written with `ensure_ascii=False` raises `UnicodeEncodeError` (headers are latin-1). base64url avoids
  both. Messages are cut to 500 characters (cookie limit is 4096 bytes).
- **Who consumes the flash.** `ContextMiddleware` decodes the cookie only for a `GET` without
  `HX-Request: true`, and deletes it only when that response is `200` + `text/html` and does not itself
  set a new `warden_flash`. So an htmx poll (camera cards poll every 5 s) racing the redirect cannot eat
  the message, a redirect chain (`/login` -> `/` when logged in) keeps it until a page renders, and a
  page that sets a newer flash keeps the newer one. Consequence for later tasks: **never call
  `set_flash` on a response to an htmx request that swaps a fragment**; render the message inside the
  fragment, or pair `set_flash` with an `HX-Redirect` / plain 303 so a full page load follows.
- **Templates and globals.** `templates.env.globals["app_version"] = __version__` lives on the shared
  instance. `base.html` must not *call* any function global, because `routes/events.py`,
  `routes/recordings.py` and `routes/clips.py` (owned by RW-3, not touched here) still render `base.html`
  through their own environments; calling an undefined global raises `UndefinedError`. That is also why
  the footer reads `{{ app_version | default(request.app.version) }}` (`create_app` passes
  `version=__version__` to `FastAPI`, web/app.py:73-78) and why the flash partial reads
  `request.state.flash` rather than a global.
- **Page header usage.** Inside `{% block content %}`: `{% set title = "..." %}`, optional
  `{% set subtitle = "..." %}`, optional block assignment
  `{% set header_actions %}<a href="..." role="button">...</a>{% endset %}`, then
  `{% include "partials/page_header.html" %}`. Includes see block-local `set` variables, and a block
  assignment is `Markup` under autoescape, so the action HTML is not escaped while `title` is.
  (The variable is `header_actions`, not `actions`, because RW-3's Actions page passes `actions` in its
  context.)
- **Ownership.** `routes/events.py`, `routes/recordings.py`, `routes/clips.py`, `events/*`,
  `recordings/*`, `clips/*` are RW-3's and stay untouched. `cameras.py`, `zones.py`, `onvif.py` (and
  RW-3's `alerts.py`) already import `templates` from `_common`; six modules switch here.

  ```python
  # src/rtsp_warden/web/flash.py
  FLASH_COOKIE = "warden_flash"
  FLASH_MAX_AGE_SECONDS = 60
  MAX_FLASH_CHARS = 500
  FlashLevel = Literal["info", "success", "error"]
  FLASH_LEVELS: tuple[str, ...] = ("info", "success", "error")
  @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
  class Flash:
      message: str
      level: str            # always one of FLASH_LEVELS
  def encode_flash(message: str, level: str = "info") -> str
  def decode_flash(raw: str | None) -> Flash | None

  # src/rtsp_warden/web/routes/_common.py
  templates: Jinja2Templates          # the one instance; templates.env.globals["app_version"] == __version__
  def set_flash(response: Response, message: str, level: FlashLevel = "info") -> None
      # cookie warden_flash, Max-Age=60, Path=/, HttpOnly, SameSite=lax
  # (get_cfg, get_config_path, find_camera unchanged)

  # src/rtsp_warden/web/context.py
  class ContextMiddleware(BaseHTTPMiddleware)   # now also sets request.state.flash: Flash | None
  ```
  Templates and CSS:
  - `base.html`: `<nav class="container-fluid site-nav">`; nav links carry `aria-current="page"` when
    `request.url.path` starts with their href (Dashboard: path == "/"); items: Dashboard, Cameras,

    `<details class="dropdown">` account menu with API Tokens and the Log out form;
    `{% include "partials/flash.html" %}` is the first line inside `<main class="container-fluid">`;
    footer `rtsp-warden v{{ app_version | default(request.app.version) }}`.
  - `partials/flash.html`: renders `<div id="flash" class="flash flash-{level}" role="status|alert">message</div>` (role `alert` for `error`) when `request.state.flash` is set.
  - `partials/page_header.html`: variables `title` (required), `subtitle` (optional), `header_actions` (optional block assignment); renders `<header class="page-header"><hgroup><h1>..</h1><p>..</p></hgroup><div class="page-actions">..</div></header>`.
  - `warden.css` classes: `.site-nav`, `.page-header`, `.page-actions`, `.flash`, `.flash-info` (default colours of `.flash`), `.flash-success`, `.flash-error`, `.table-wrap`, `.status-badge` (coloured by the existing `.status-running|restarting|failed|idle|waiting|unknown`), `.small`, `.sr-only`, `.field`, `a.button` (+ `.secondary`, `.outline`), `.status-dot.status-idle|waiting|unknown|stopped` (grey dot), and `@media (max-width: 600px)` rules for `.grid`, `table`/`th`/`td`, `.page-header` and `form > button[type="submit"]`.

```python
"""UI shell: one Jinja2Templates instance, flash messages, nav, page header, CSS primitives."""

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import importlib
import json
import re
import string
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from starlette.responses import Response

from rtsp_warden import __version__
from rtsp_warden.config import AppConfig, CameraConfig
from rtsp_warden.web.app import create_app
from rtsp_warden.web.config import WebSettings
from rtsp_warden.web.flash import (
    FLASH_COOKIE,
    MAX_FLASH_CHARS,
    Flash,
    decode_flash,
    encode_flash,
)
from rtsp_warden.web.paths import STATIC_DIR
from rtsp_warden.web.routes import _common
from rtsp_warden.web.routes._common import set_flash, templates

ROUTES_DIR = Path(_common.__file__).parent

# Modules that render through the shared instance after this task.
SHARED_TEMPLATE_MODULES = [
    "auth",
    "cameras",
    "dashboard",
    "health",
    "onvif",
    "settings",
    "tokens",
    "users",
    "zones",
]

# Route modules owned by the detection track (RW-3), which converts or deletes them.
RW3_OWNED_ROUTE_MODULES = {"clips.py", "events.py", "recordings.py"}


def _login(client: TestClient) -> None:
    client.get("/login")
    token = client.cookies.get("warden_csrf", "")
    r = client.post(
        "/login",
        data={"username": "admin", "password": "testpass123", "csrf_token": token},
        follow_redirects=False,
    )
    assert r.status_code == 303


@pytest.fixture
def app(db_with_user: str, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> FastAPI:
    """App with one camera, no runtime, and two test-only routes that set a flash."""
    monkeypatch.setenv("WARDEN_AUTH_ENABLED", "true")
    cfg = AppConfig(cameras=[CameraConfig(name="front", main_url="rtsp://u:p@h/m")])
    app = create_app(WebSettings(), cfg=cfg, runtime_provider=lambda: None)

    @app.get("/test-flash/{level}")
    async def _flash_then_redirect(level: str) -> RedirectResponse:
        response = RedirectResponse(url="/cameras", status_code=303)
        set_flash(response, f"Saved <b>{level}</b>", level)
        return response

    @app.get("/test-flash-page")
    async def _page_that_sets_a_new_flash() -> HTMLResponse:
        response = HTMLResponse("<p>page</p>")
        set_flash(response, "newer message", "error")
        return response

    return app


@pytest.fixture
def client(app: FastAPI) -> TestClient:
    c = TestClient(app)
    _login(c)
    return c


# --- one templates instance -------------------------------------------------


@pytest.mark.parametrize("module_name", SHARED_TEMPLATE_MODULES)
def test_route_module_renders_through_shared_templates(module_name: str) -> None:
    module = importlib.import_module(f"rtsp_warden.web.routes.{module_name}")
    assert module.templates is templates
    assert not hasattr(module, "_templates")


def test_only_rw3_route_modules_still_build_their_own_templates() -> None:
    offenders = {
        path.name
        for path in ROUTES_DIR.glob("*.py")
        if path.name != "_common.py" and "Jinja2Templates(" in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    }
    assert offenders <= RW3_OWNED_ROUTE_MODULES


def test_app_version_is_a_template_global() -> None:
    assert templates.env.globals["app_version"] == __version__


# --- flash codec ------------------------------------------------------------


def test_flash_round_trip_keeps_quotes_commas_and_non_ascii() -> None:
    message = 'Camera "Tür ✓" saved, restart; done'
    raw = encode_flash(message, "success")
    assert set(raw) <= set(string.ascii_letters + string.digits + "-_")
    assert decode_flash(raw) == Flash(message=message, level="success")


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "raw",
    [
        None,
        "",
        "%%%",
        "not base64 ✓",
        base64.urlsafe_b64encode(b"not json").decode(),
        base64.urlsafe_b64encode(b"[1, 2]").decode(),
        base64.urlsafe_b64encode(b'{"m": 5, "l": "info"}').decode(),
        base64.urlsafe_b64encode(b'{"m": "", "l": "info"}').decode(),
    ],
)
def test_decode_flash_rejects_garbage(raw: str | None) -> None:
    assert decode_flash(raw) is None


def test_unknown_level_becomes_info() -> None:
    forged = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(
        json.dumps({"m": "hi", "l": 'x" onclick="alert(1)'}).encode()
    ).decode()
    assert decode_flash(forged) == Flash(message="hi", level="info")
    assert decode_flash(encode_flash("hi", "danger")) == Flash(message="hi", level="info")


def test_long_flash_message_is_truncated() -> None:
    flash = decode_flash(encode_flash("a" * (MAX_FLASH_CHARS + 100)))
    assert flash is not None
    assert len(flash.message) == MAX_FLASH_CHARS


def test_set_flash_cookie_attributes() -> None:
    response = Response()
    set_flash(response, "Saved", "success")
    header = response.headers["set-cookie"]
    assert header.startswith(f"{FLASH_COOKIE}=")
    assert "Max-Age=60" in header
    assert "Path=/" in header
    assert "HttpOnly" in header
    assert "SameSite=lax" in header


# --- flash through the middleware -------------------------------------------


def test_flash_is_shown_once_after_redirect(client: TestClient) -> None:
    r = client.get("/test-flash/success")
    assert r.status_code == 200
    assert r.url.path == "/cameras"
    assert '<div id="flash" class="flash flash-success" role="status">' in r.text
    assert "Saved &lt;b&gt;success&lt;/b&gt;" in r.text
    assert client.cookies.get(FLASH_COOKIE) is None

    again = client.get("/cameras")
    assert 'id="flash"' not in again.text


def test_error_flash_uses_alert_role(client: TestClient) -> None:
    r = client.get("/test-flash/error")
    assert '<div id="flash" class="flash flash-error" role="alert">' in r.text


def test_htmx_fragment_does_not_consume_pending_flash(client: TestClient) -> None:
    r = client.get("/test-flash/info", follow_redirects=False)
    assert r.status_code == 303
    assert client.cookies.get(FLASH_COOKIE)

    fragment = client.get("/health/partial", headers={"HX-Request": "true"})
    assert fragment.status_code == 200
    assert 'id="flash"' not in fragment.text
    assert client.cookies.get(FLASH_COOKIE)

    page = client.get("/cameras")
    assert "flash-info" in page.text
    assert client.cookies.get(FLASH_COOKIE) is None


def test_redirect_does_not_consume_pending_flash(client: TestClient) -> None:
    client.get("/test-flash/info", follow_redirects=False)
    # A logged-in GET /login answers 303 -> "/": the flash must wait for the page.
    r = client.get("/login", follow_redirects=False)
    assert r.status_code == 303
    assert client.cookies.get(FLASH_COOKIE)

    page = client.get("/")
    assert "flash-info" in page.text
    assert client.cookies.get(FLASH_COOKIE) is None


def test_page_that_sets_a_new_flash_keeps_it(client: TestClient) -> None:
    client.get("/test-flash/info", follow_redirects=False)
    r = client.get("/test-flash-page")
    flash_headers = [
        v for v in r.headers.get_list("set-cookie") if v.startswith(f"{FLASH_COOKIE}=")
    ]
    assert len(flash_headers) == 1
    assert "Max-Age=60" in flash_headers[0]
    assert decode_flash(client.cookies.get(FLASH_COOKIE)) == Flash("newer message", "error")


def test_logout_flash_on_login_page(client: TestClient) -> None:
    token = client.cookies.get("warden_csrf", "")
    r = client.post("/logout", data={"csrf_token": token})
    assert r.url.path == "/login"
    html = r.text
    assert "You have been logged out." in html
    assert "hx-post" not in html
    assert html.count("<form") == 1  # only the login form; the flash adds none

    again = client.get("/login")
    assert "You have been logged out." not in again.text


# --- footer, health partial, nav --------------------------------------------


@pytest.mark.parametrize("path", ["/", "/cameras", "/settings", "/users", "/api-tokens", "/health"])
def test_footer_shows_version(client: TestClient, path: str) -> None:
    r = client.get(path)
    assert r.status_code == 200
    assert f"rtsp-warden v{__version__}</small>" in r.text


def test_footer_shows_version_on_login_page(app: FastAPI) -> None:
    html = TestClient(app).get("/login").text
    assert f"rtsp-warden v{__version__}</small>" in html


def test_health_partial_keeps_version_cell(app: FastAPI) -> None:
    r = TestClient(app).get("/health/partial")
    assert r.status_code == 200
    assert f"<td>{__version__}</td>" in r.text


def test_nav_marks_current_section(client: TestClient) -> None:
    html = client.get("/cameras/front").text
    assert '<a href="/cameras" aria-current="page">Cameras</a>' in html
    assert '<a href="/events">Events</a>' in html
    assert '<a href="/">Dashboard</a>' in html

    home = client.get("/").text
    assert '<a href="/" aria-current="page">Dashboard</a>' in home
    assert '<a href="/cameras">Cameras</a>' in home


def test_nav_has_wrap_class_health_link_and_account_dropdown(client: TestClient) -> None:
    html = client.get("/cameras").text
    assert '<nav class="container-fluid site-nav">' in html
    assert '<a href="/health">Health</a>' in html
    assert '<details class="dropdown">' in html
    assert '<a href="/api-tokens">API Tokens</a>' in html
    assert 'action="/logout"' in html


# --- page header partial ----------------------------------------------------


def test_page_header_with_subtitle_and_actions() -> None:
    html = templates.env.from_string(
        '{% set title = "Cameras" %}{% set subtitle = "2 configured" %}'
        '{% set header_actions %}<a href="/cameras/new" role="button">Add camera</a>{% endset %}'
        '{% include "partials/page_header.html" %}'
    ).render()
    assert '<header class="page-header">' in html
    assert "<h1>Cameras</h1>" in html
    assert "<p>2 configured</p>" in html
    assert (
        '<div class="page-actions"><a href="/cameras/new" role="button">Add camera</a></div>'
        in html
    )


def test_page_header_title_only_and_escaped() -> None:
    html = templates.get_template("partials/page_header.html").render(title="<b>Users</b>")
    assert "<h1>&lt;b&gt;Users&lt;/b&gt;</h1>" in html
    assert "<p>" not in html
    assert "page-actions" not in html


# --- CSS primitives ---------------------------------------------------------


def test_css_defines_layout_primitives(app: FastAPI) -> None:
    css = (STATIC_DIR / "css" / "warden.css").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for needle in (
        ".site-nav,\n.site-nav > ul {\n  flex-wrap: wrap;",
        ".page-header {",
        ".page-actions {",
        ".flash {",
        ".flash-success {",
        ".flash-error {",
        ".table-wrap {\n  overflow-x: auto;",
        ".status-badge {",
        ".small {",
        ".sr-only {",
        ".field {",
        "a.button {",
        ".status-dot.status-idle",
        ".status-dot.status-waiting",
        "@media (max-width: 600px) {",
        "grid-template-columns: 1fr !important;",
    ):
        assert needle in css, needle
    # Status colours used on text labels must not paint a background box.
    assert re.search(r"^\.status-(ok|error|unknown|stopped)\s*\{", css, re.MULTILINE) is None
    served = TestClient(app).get("/static/css/warden.css")
    assert served.status_code == 200
    assert ".flash-error" in served.text
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the flash codec module**

Create `/home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2/src/rtsp_warden/web/flash.py`:

```python
"""One-shot flash messages carried across a redirect in a short-lived cookie.

A route calls ``set_flash(response, message, level)`` (``web/routes/_common.py``) on the
response it returns, usually a 303 redirect. ``ContextMiddleware`` (``web/context.py``)
decodes the cookie on the next full-page GET into ``request.state.flash``,
``partials/flash.html`` renders it, and the middleware deletes the cookie on that
response, so the message is shown exactly once.

The cookie value is base64url (no padding) of the JSON object ``{"m": message, "l": level}``.
Plain JSON is not a valid RFC 6265 cookie value (quotes, commas, spaces) and response
headers must be latin-1, so the encoded form keeps any message text safe.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal

FLASH_COOKIE = "warden_flash"
FLASH_MAX_AGE_SECONDS = 60
MAX_FLASH_CHARS = 500

FlashLevel = Literal["info", "success", "error"]
FLASH_LEVELS: tuple[str, ...] = ("info", "success", "error")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Flash:
    """A decoded flash message. ``level`` is always one of FLASH_LEVELS."""

    message: str
    level: str


def _normalise_level(level: object) -> str:
    """Return ``level`` when it is a known level, else ``"info"``."""
    return level if isinstance(level, str) and level in FLASH_LEVELS else "info"


def encode_flash(message: str, level: str = "info") -> str:
    """Return the cookie value for a flash message (message cut to MAX_FLASH_CHARS)."""
    payload = json.dumps({"m": message[:MAX_FLASH_CHARS], "l": _normalise_level(level)})
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(payload.encode("utf-8")).decode("ascii").rstrip("=")


def decode_flash(raw: str | None) -> Flash | None:
    """Parse a cookie value made by encode_flash; None when absent or malformed."""
    if not raw:
        return None
    try:
        data = json.loads(base64.urlsafe_b64decode(raw + "=" * (-len(raw) % 4)))
    except ValueError:  # binascii.Error, UnicodeDecodeError and JSONDecodeError
        return None
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        return None
    message = data.get("m")
    if not isinstance(message, str) or not message:
        return None
    return Flash(message=message[:MAX_FLASH_CHARS], level=_normalise_level(data.get("l")))
```

```python
"""Helpers shared by route modules: templates, flash messages, config access, camera lookup."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import HTTPException, Request
from starlette.responses import Response
from starlette.templating import Jinja2Templates

from ... import __version__
from ...config import AppConfig, CameraConfig
from ..flash import FLASH_COOKIE, FLASH_MAX_AGE_SECONDS, FlashLevel, encode_flash
from ..paths import TEMPLATES_DIR

# The one Jinja2Templates instance. Every route module renders through it, so a global
# registered here (the footer's app_version) is visible on every page.
templates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(TEMPLATES_DIR))
templates.env.globals["app_version"] = __version__


def set_flash(response: Response, message: str, level: FlashLevel = "info") -> None:
    """Attach a one-shot message that the next full-page GET shows (see web/flash.py)."""
    response.set_cookie(
        key=FLASH_COOKIE,
        value=encode_flash(message, level),
        max_age=FLASH_MAX_AGE_SECONDS,
        path="/",
        httponly=True,
        samesite="lax",
    )
```

- [ ] **Step 12: Teach `ContextMiddleware` to read and clear the flash cookie**

Replace the whole of `/home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2/src/rtsp_warden/web/context.py`. Current file (35 lines):

```python
"""Request context middleware: resolves the current user and CSRF token.

On every request, this middleware:
  1. Resolves ``current_user`` via the auth bridge and stores it on
     ``request.state.current_user``.
  2. Propagates the CSRF token (set by CSRFMiddleware) onto
     ``request.state.csrf_token`` for template access.

Templates can then use ``{{ request.state.current_user }}`` and
``{{ request.state.csrf_token }}`` without per-route plumbing.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from starlette.middleware.base import BaseHTTPMiddleware, RequestResponseEndpoint
from starlette.requests import Request
from starlette.responses import Response

from .auth_bridge import get_current_user_from_request


class ContextMiddleware(BaseHTTPMiddleware):
    """Starlette middleware that populates request.state with auth context."""

    async def dispatch(self, request: Request, call_next: RequestResponseEndpoint) -> Response:
        # Resolve the current user (may be None for unauthenticated requests)
        current_user = get_current_user_from_request(request)
        request.state.current_user = current_user

        # Propagate CSRF token if not already set by CSRFMiddleware
        if not hasattr(request.state, "csrf_token"):
            request.state.csrf_token = request.cookies.get("warden_csrf", "")

        response: Response = await call_next(request)
        return response
```

New file:

```python
"""Request context middleware: current user, CSRF token and flash message.

On every request, this middleware:
  1. Resolves ``current_user`` via the auth bridge and stores it on
     ``request.state.current_user``.
  2. Propagates the CSRF token (set by CSRFMiddleware) onto
     ``request.state.csrf_token`` for template access.
  3. On full-page GETs, decodes a pending ``warden_flash`` cookie onto
     ``request.state.flash`` (None otherwise) and deletes the cookie once an
     HTML page was rendered with it, so the message shows exactly once.

Templates can then use ``{{ request.state.current_user }}``,
``{{ request.state.csrf_token }}`` and ``{{ request.state.flash }}`` without
per-route plumbing.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from starlette.middleware.base import BaseHTTPMiddleware, RequestResponseEndpoint
from starlette.requests import Request
from starlette.responses import Response

from .auth_bridge import get_current_user_from_request
from .flash import FLASH_COOKIE, decode_flash


def _shows_flash(request: Request) -> bool:
    """Only a full-page GET shows a flash; htmx fragments and form posts leave it pending."""
    return request.method == "GET" and request.headers.get("hx-request") != "true"


def _is_html_page(response: Response) -> bool:
    """True for a 200 HTML response (a redirect or an error does not consume the flash)."""
    content_type = response.headers.get("content-type", "")
    return response.status_code == 200 and content_type.startswith("text/html")


def _sets_cookie(response: Response, name: str) -> bool:
    """True when the response already sets the cookie ``name`` (a newer flash)."""
    prefix = f"{name}="
    return any(value.startswith(prefix) for value in response.headers.getlist("set-cookie"))


class ContextMiddleware(BaseHTTPMiddleware):
    """Starlette middleware that populates request.state with auth context."""

    async def dispatch(self, request: Request, call_next: RequestResponseEndpoint) -> Response:
        # Resolve the current user (may be None for unauthenticated requests)
        current_user = get_current_user_from_request(request)
        request.state.current_user = current_user

        # Propagate CSRF token if not already set by CSRFMiddleware
        if not hasattr(request.state, "csrf_token"):
            request.state.csrf_token = request.cookies.get("warden_csrf", "")

        raw_flash = request.cookies.get(FLASH_COOKIE) if _shows_flash(request) else None
        request.state.flash = decode_flash(raw_flash)

        response: Response = await call_next(request)

        if (
            raw_flash is not None
            and _is_html_page(response)
            and not _sets_cookie(response, FLASH_COOKIE)
        ):
            response.delete_cookie(FLASH_COOKIE, path="/")
        return response
```

```css
/* --- Layout primitives (UI shell) --- */

/* Top nav: wrap onto a second line instead of overflowing; mark the current page. */
.site-nav,
.site-nav > ul {
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}
.site-nav a[aria-current="page"] {
  font-weight: 600;
}
.site-nav details.dropdown form {
  margin: 0;
}
.site-nav details.dropdown button {
  width: 100%;
  margin: 0;
}

/* Page header (partials/page_header.html): title and subtitle left, actions right. */
.page-header {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 0.5rem 1rem;
  margin-bottom: var(--pico-spacing);
}
.page-header hgroup {
  margin-bottom: 0;
}
.page-actions {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 0.5rem;
}
.page-actions > * {
  margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* Flash messages (partials/flash.html). */
.flash {
  --flash-accent: #1565c0;
  --flash-bg: rgba(21, 101, 192, 0.1);
  margin-bottom: var(--pico-spacing);
  padding: 0.75rem 1rem;
  border-left: 0.25rem solid var(--flash-accent);
  border-radius: var(--pico-border-radius);
  background: var(--flash-bg);
}
.flash-success {
  --flash-accent: #2e7d32;
  --flash-bg: rgba(46, 125, 50, 0.12);
}
.flash-error {
  --flash-accent: #c62828;
  --flash-bg: rgba(198, 40, 40, 0.12);
}

/* Wide tables scroll sideways inside their box instead of widening the page. */
.table-wrap {
  overflow-x: auto;
  margin-bottom: var(--pico-spacing);
}
.table-wrap > table {
  margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* Status badge: outlined pill; colour from .status-running/-restarting/-failed/-idle/-waiting. */
.status-badge {
  display: inline-block;
  padding: 0.05rem 0.5rem;
  border: 1px solid currentColor;
  border-radius: 1rem;
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  line-height: 1.4;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

/* Small text, and compact buttons with class="small". */
.small {
  font-size: 0.875em;
}
:is(button, [role="button"], [type="submit"], a.button).small {
  --pico-form-element-spacing-vertical: 0.25rem;
  --pico-form-element-spacing-horizontal: 0.6rem;
  font-size: 0.875rem;
}

/* Visually hidden but read by screen readers. */
.sr-only {
  position: absolute;
  width: 1px;
  height: 1px;
  margin: -1px;
  padding: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  clip: rect(0, 0, 0, 0);
  white-space: nowrap;
  border: 0;
}

/* One labelled form control plus optional help text. */
.field {
  margin-bottom: var(--pico-spacing);
}
.field :is(input, select, textarea) {
  margin-bottom: 0.25rem;
}
.field > small {
  display: block;
  color: var(--pico-muted-color);
}

/* Links with class="button" (Pico 2 itself styles only role="button"). */
a.button {
  display: inline-block;
  padding: var(--pico-form-element-spacing-vertical) var(--pico-form-element-spacing-horizontal);
  border: var(--pico-border-width) solid var(--pico-primary-border);
  border-radius: var(--pico-border-radius);
  background-color: var(--pico-primary-background);
  color: var(--pico-primary-inverse);
  font-weight: var(--pico-font-weight);
  line-height: var(--pico-line-height);
  text-align: center;
  text-decoration: none;
  cursor: pointer;
}
a.button.secondary {
  border-color: var(--pico-secondary-border);
  background-color: var(--pico-secondary-background);
  color: var(--pico-secondary-inverse);
}
a.button.outline {
  background-color: transparent;
  color: var(--pico-primary);
}
a.button.outline.secondary {
  color: var(--pico-secondary);
}

/* Dark theme: lighter status colours so labels stay readable. */
[data-theme="dark"] .status-running {
  color: #81c784;
}
[data-theme="dark"] .status-restarting {
  color: #ffb74d;
}
[data-theme="dark"] .status-failed,
[data-theme="dark"] .camera-error {
  color: #ef9a9a;
}

/* Phones. Pico already stacks .grid below 768 px; !important also beats inline
   grid-template-columns. Tables get tighter cells, form submit buttons go full width. */
@media (max-width: 600px) {
  .grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr !important;
  }
  table {
    font-size: 0.875rem;
  }
  th,
  td {
    padding: 0.4rem 0.5rem;
  }
  .page-header {
    align-items: flex-start;
  }
  form > button[type="submit"] {
    width: 100%;
  }
}
```

### Task 2: htmx forms stop swapping whole pages into themselves (users, tokens)

**Why.** Every htmx form on the users and API-token pages swaps a whole HTML page into itself (ui-shell map, inconsistency 17): `users/list.html:34-35` and `43-44` post to routes that answer 303 → `/users`, htmx follows the redirect and nests the full `/users` page (nav, main, footer) inside the form; `users/new.html:23-24` and `users/reset_password.html:22-23` nest the 200 page they get back; `tokens/list.html:23-26` and `64-68` swap the full page into the FIRST `<article>` (`hx-target="article"`). Commit 73ee25b fixed the same bug on `/login` by making it a plain form.

**Design (three rules, applied to exactly these pages):**
1. A form whose answer is a whole new page state (create user, reset password: both show a one-time password) is a **plain HTML form**: no `hx-*` attributes. The routes already return the page with 200, so `routes/users.py` `create_new_user` and `reset_password_submit` do not change.
2. A form that changes one region of the page it sits on (toggle-admin and delete in the users table; create and revoke on the tokens page) targets an **id'd wrapper** (`#users-table`, `#tokens-panel`) with `hx-swap="outerHTML"`. For an htmx request (`HX-Request: true`, read by the new `_common.is_htmx`) the route returns only that wrapper's partial with 200, errors included inside it (htmx 2.0.4 does not swap 4xx responses, ui-shell G2c). Without htmx every route keeps the status code `tests/test_admin.py` pins: user toggle and delete 303 → `/users` (now with a Task 1 flash message) and 400/404 on error; token create and revoke 200 with the whole page, 404 for an unknown token.
3. Every form keeps its hidden `csrf_token` input: `users.py` and `tokens.py` call `check_csrf_form` (`web/csrf.py:102-109`), which reads only the form field (ui-shell G4). The per-form `hx-headers` X-CSRF-Token copies go (warden.js adds the header to every htmx request; inconsistency 16).

While rewriting these templates the task also removes their inline `grid-template-columns` styles (inconsistency 14), replaces their `<ins data-color=...>` alerts with Task 1's `.flash` classes (inconsistency 23), wraps both tables in Task 1's `.table-wrap`, and fixes `partials/password_reveal.html` (ui-shell G18): it embeds the one-time secret inside a single-quoted JS string (`:value="'{{ secret_value }}'"`, `writeText('{{ secret_value }}')`), so an admin-typed password containing `'` breaks the widget or runs script. The new partial puts the secret only in the escaped `value` attribute and copies from `$refs.secret`.

**Ownership.** Every file here is on the RW-2 list in decisions.md (`users/*`, `tokens/*`, `partials/password_reveal.html`, new partials, `routes/users.py`, `routes/tokens.py`, `routes/_common.py`, `warden.css`). No RW-3 file and no ruling beyond that list is involved. None of the skeleton's five Review Focus candidates belongs to this task.

**Task 1 dependency.** This task runs after Task 1, which (per its Interfaces) adds `set_flash` to `web/routes/_common.py`, switches `routes/users.py` and `routes/tokens.py` to `from ._common import templates` (deleting their local `_templates = Jinja2Templates(...)`), and defines the CSS classes `.table-wrap`, `.flash`, `.flash-success`, `.flash-error`, `.small`. Line numbers below are master's (RW-0 does not touch these files). Because Task 1 edits the imports of `users.py` and `tokens.py`, Steps 10 and 17 replace those two files whole; Step 1 checks that Task 1 changed nothing else in them.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing users tests**

Create `tests/test_users_tokens_ui.py`. It uses the conftest `db_with_user` fixture (admin `admin` / `testpass123`, user id 1) and `monkeypatch` for `WARDEN_AUTH_ENABLED` (tests-conventions G6: never set `os.environ` at import). htmx is emulated with the `HX-Request: true` header, as `tests/test_auth_redirect.py:25-28` does.

```python
"""htmx forms on the users and API-token pages swap fragments, not whole pages.

Toggle-admin and delete on /users, and create and revoke on /api-tokens, are
htmx forms: with ``HX-Request: true`` the routes return only the partial the
form targets. Without htmx they keep their old status codes (303 for user
toggle/delete, 200 full page for tokens). The new-user and reset-password pages
are plain forms because their response is a whole page.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from starlette.requests import Request

from rtsp_warden.auth import hash_password
from rtsp_warden.db import create_user, get_user_by_id
from rtsp_warden.web.app import create_app
from rtsp_warden.web.config import WebSettings
from rtsp_warden.web.rate_limit import get_login_limiter

HX = {"HX-Request": "true"}
_FORM_RE = re.compile(r"<form\b.*?</form>", re.DOTALL)


def _login(client: TestClient) -> None:
    client.get("/login")
    token = client.cookies.get("warden_csrf", "")
    r = client.post(
        "/login",
        data={"username": "admin", "password": "testpass123", "csrf_token": token},
        follow_redirects=False,
    )
    assert r.status_code == 303


def _post(client: TestClient, url: str, data: dict | None = None, *, htmx: bool = False):
    """POST a form with the hidden csrf_token field, optionally as htmx."""
    form = dict(data or {})
    form["csrf_token"] = client.cookies.get("warden_csrf", "")
    headers = dict(HX) if htmx else {}
    return client.post(url, data=form, headers=headers, follow_redirects=False)


def _assert_fragment(body: str, root_id: str) -> None:
    """The body is one partial rooted at ``<div id=root_id>``, not a page."""
    assert body.lstrip().startswith(f'<div id="{root_id}"')
    assert "<html" not in body
    assert "<nav" not in body
    assert "<footer" not in body


def _form_with_action(html: str, action: str) -> str:
    for form in _FORM_RE.findall(html):
        if f'action="{action}"' in form:
            return form
    raise AssertionError(f"no form with action={action!r}")


@pytest.fixture
def client(db_with_user: str, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> TestClient:
    monkeypatch.setenv("WARDEN_AUTH_ENABLED", "true")
    get_login_limiter()._attempts.clear()
    c = TestClient(create_app(WebSettings()))
    _login(c)
    return c


def test_is_htmx_reads_the_hx_request_header() -> None:
    from rtsp_warden.web.routes._common import is_htmx

    def req(headers: list[tuple[bytes, bytes]]) -> Request:
        return Request({"type": "http", "method": "POST", "path": "/", "headers": headers})

    assert is_htmx(req([(b"hx-request", b"true")])) is True
    assert is_htmx(req([])) is False
    assert is_htmx(req([(b"hx-request", b"false")])) is False


class TestUsersTable:
    def test_users_page_forms_target_the_table_wrapper(self, client: TestClient) -> None:
        create_user("bob", hash_password("bobpass12345"), is_admin=False)
        html = client.get("/users").text
        assert 'id="users-table"' in html
        htmx_forms = [f for f in _FORM_RE.findall(html) if "hx-post" in f]
        assert len(htmx_forms) == 4  # 2 users x (toggle-admin + delete)
        for form in htmx_forms:
            assert 'hx-target="#users-table"' in form
            assert 'hx-swap="outerHTML"' in form
        assert "hx-headers" not in html
        assert 'style="grid-template-columns' not in html

    def test_toggle_admin_htmx_returns_the_table_partial(self, client: TestClient) -> None:
        create_user("bob", hash_password("bobpass12345"), is_admin=False)
        r = _post(client, "/users/2/toggle-admin", htmx=True)
        assert r.status_code == 200
        _assert_fragment(r.text, "users-table")
        assert "bob is now an admin." in r.text
        assert get_user_by_id(2).role == "admin"

    def test_toggle_admin_without_htmx_redirects_with_flash(self, client: TestClient) -> None:
        create_user("bob", hash_password("bobpass12345"), is_admin=False)
        r = _post(client, "/users/2/toggle-admin")
        assert r.status_code == 303
        assert r.headers["location"] == "/users"
        assert any(c.startswith("warden_flash=") for c in r.headers.get_list("set-cookie"))

    def test_delete_user_htmx_returns_the_table_without_the_row(self, client: TestClient) -> None:
        create_user("carol", hash_password("carolpass1234"), is_admin=False)
        r = _post(client, "/users/2/delete", htmx=True)
        assert r.status_code == 200
        _assert_fragment(r.text, "users-table")
        assert "User carol deleted." in r.text
        assert "/users/2/delete" not in r.text
        assert get_user_by_id(2) is None

    def test_delete_self_htmx_shows_the_error_inside_the_table(self, client: TestClient) -> None:
        r = _post(client, "/users/1/delete", htmx=True)
        assert r.status_code == 200
        _assert_fragment(r.text, "users-table")
        assert "You cannot delete your own account." in r.text
        assert get_user_by_id(1) is not None

    def test_toggle_unknown_user_htmx_shows_not_found(self, client: TestClient) -> None:
        r = _post(client, "/users/99/toggle-admin", htmx=True)
        assert r.status_code == 200
        _assert_fragment(r.text, "users-table")
        assert "User not found." in r.text

    def test_new_user_and_reset_password_are_plain_forms(self, client: TestClient) -> None:
        for url in ("/users/new", "/users/1/reset-password"):
            form = _form_with_action(client.get(url).text, url)
            assert 'method="POST"' in form
            assert "hx-" not in form
            assert 'style="grid-template-columns' not in form
```

```html
{#
  Users table. Included by users/list.html and returned on its own by the
  toggle-admin and delete routes (routes/users.py) for htmx requests; the
  forms swap it over #users-table.

  Expects: users, current_user_id. Optional: notice, error.
#}
<div id="users-table">
  {% if notice %}
  <p class="flash flash-success" role="status">{{ notice }}</p>
  {% endif %}
  {% if error %}
  <p class="flash flash-error" role="alert">{{ error }}</p>
  {% endif %}
  <div class="table-wrap">
    <table>
      <thead>
        <tr>
          <th>Username</th>
          <th>Role</th>
          <th>Created</th>
          <th>Last Login</th>
          <th>Actions</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {% for u in users %}
        <tr>
          <td>{{ u.username }}</td>
          <td>{{ u.role }}</td>
          <td>{{ u.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M') if u.created_at else '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ u.last_login_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M') if u.last_login_at else 'Never' }}</td>
          <td>
            <div class="row-actions">
              <a href="/users/{{ u.id }}/reset-password" role="button" class="secondary outline small">Reset Password</a>
              <form method="POST" action="/users/{{ u.id }}/toggle-admin"
                    hx-post="/users/{{ u.id }}/toggle-admin"
                    hx-target="#users-table"
                    hx-swap="outerHTML">
                <input type="hidden" name="csrf_token" value="{{ request.state.csrf_token if request.state.csrf_token else '' }}">
                <button type="submit" class="secondary outline small"
                        {% if u.id == current_user_id %}disabled title="Cannot change your own role"{% endif %}>
                  {{ 'Demote' if u.role == 'admin' else 'Make Admin' }}
                </button>
              </form>
              <form method="POST" action="/users/{{ u.id }}/delete"
                    hx-post="/users/{{ u.id }}/delete"
                    hx-target="#users-table"
                    hx-swap="outerHTML"
                    hx-confirm="Delete user {{ u.username }}? This cannot be undone.">
                <input type="hidden" name="csrf_token" value="{{ request.state.csrf_token if request.state.csrf_token else '' }}">
                <button type="submit" class="outline small contrast"
                        {% if u.id == current_user_id %}disabled title="Cannot delete your own account"{% endif %}>
                  Delete
                </button>
              </form>
            </div>
          </td>
        </tr>
        {% endfor %}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  </div>
</div>
```

```html
{% extends "base.html" %}

{% block title %}New User{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<article>
  <h1>Create New User</h1>

  {% if generated_password %}
  <p class="flash flash-success" role="status">
    User <strong>{{ username }}</strong> created successfully.
  </p>

           minlength="3" maxlength="32" pattern="[a-zA-Z0-9_]+"
           placeholder="3-32 chars, letters, digits, underscores">

    <label for="password">Password</label>
    <div role="group" x-data="{ pw: '' }">
      <input type="text" id="password" name="password" x-model="pw"
             placeholder="Leave blank to auto-generate" autocomplete="new-password">
      <button type="button" class="secondary"
              @click="pw = Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12)), b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('').slice(0, 16)">

      Admin privileges
    </label>

    <div class="grid">
      <button type="submit">Create User</button>
      <a href="/users" role="button" class="secondary">Cancel</a>
    </div>
  </form>
  {% endif %}
</article>
{% endblock %}
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Return the users partial to htmx from toggle-admin and delete**

Current master `routes/users.py:11-13` and `25-31` (Task 1 already replaced the `Jinja2Templates` lines with `from ._common import templates`):
```python
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse
from starlette.templating import Jinja2Templates
...
from ..auth_depends import CurrentUser, require_admin
from ..csrf import check_csrf_form
from ..paths import TEMPLATES_DIR

router = APIRouter(prefix="/users")

_templates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(TEMPLATES_DIR))
```

Current master `routes/users.py:193-237` (both handlers end in a bare 303, which htmx follows and swaps):
```python
@router.post("/{user_id}/delete")
async def delete_user_route(
    request: Request,
    user_id: int,
    csrf_token: str = Form(""),
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_admin),
) -> RedirectResponse:
    """Delete a user. Refuses to delete the logged-in admin."""
    if not check_csrf_form(request, csrf_token):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="CSRF token missing or invalid")

    # Refuse to delete self
    if user_id == user.user_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="You cannot delete your own account.")

    success = delete_user(user_id)
    if not success:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not found")

    return RedirectResponse(url="/users", status_code=303)


@router.post("/{user_id}/toggle-admin")
async def toggle_admin_route(
    request: Request,
    user_id: int,
    csrf_token: str = Form(""),
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_admin),
) -> RedirectResponse:
    """Toggle a user's admin status. Refuses to demote self."""
    if not check_csrf_form(request, csrf_token):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="CSRF token missing or invalid")

    # Refuse to demote self
    if user_id == user.user_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="You cannot change your own admin status.")

    target = get_user_by_id(user_id)
    if target is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not found")

    new_admin = target.role != "admin"
    set_user_admin(user_id, new_admin)

    return RedirectResponse(url="/users", status_code=303)
```

Replace the whole of `/home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2/src/rtsp_warden/web/routes/users.py` with the file below. `users_list`, `new_user_form`, `create_new_user`, `reset_password_form` and `reset_password_submit` are unchanged apart from using the shared `templates`. `-> Response` (not a union) keeps FastAPI from building a response model for the two handlers that now return either an `HTMLResponse` or a `RedirectResponse`.
```python
"""User management route handlers for the rtsp-warden web UI.

Provides admin-only CRUD for users: list, create, reset password,
delete, and toggle admin status.

The create and reset-password pages are plain HTML forms because their
response is a whole page. Toggle-admin and delete are htmx forms on the users
list: an htmx request gets ``partials/users_table.html`` back (200, errors
shown inside it), which the page swaps over ``#users-table``. Without htmx
they redirect to ``/users`` (303) with a flash message, or fail with 400/404.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request, Response
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse

from ... import auth
from ...db import (
    create_user,
    delete_user,
    get_user_by_id,
    get_user_by_username,
    list_users,
    set_user_admin,
    update_user_password,
)
from ..auth_depends import CurrentUser, require_admin
from ..csrf import check_csrf_form
from ._common import is_htmx, set_flash, templates

router = APIRouter(prefix="/users")

# Valid username: 3-32 chars, alphanumeric + underscore
_USERNAME_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{3,32}$")
_MIN_PASSWORD_LEN = 8


def _users_table(
    request: Request,
    user: CurrentUser,
    *,
    notice: str | None = None,
    error: str | None = None,
) -> HTMLResponse:
    """Render the users table partial that htmx swaps over ``#users-table``."""
    return templates.TemplateResponse(
        request,
        "partials/users_table.html",
        {
            "request": request,
            "users": list_users(),
            "current_user_id": user.user_id,
            "notice": notice,
            "error": error,
        },
    )


@router.get("", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def users_list(request: Request, user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_admin)) -> HTMLResponse:
    """Render the user list page (admin-only)."""
    all_users = list_users()
    return templates.TemplateResponse(
        request,
        "users/list.html",
        {
            "request": request,
            "users": all_users,
            "current_user_id": user.user_id,
        },
    )


@router.get("/new", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def new_user_form(
    request: Request, user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_admin)
) -> HTMLResponse:
    """Render the new user creation form."""
    return templates.TemplateResponse(
        request,
        "users/new.html",
        {
            "request": request,
            "error": None,
            "username": "",
            "is_admin": False,
            "generated_password": None,
        },
    )


@router.post("/new", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def create_new_user(
    request: Request,
    username: str = Form(""),
    password: str = Form(""),
    is_admin: str = Form(""),
    csrf_token: str = Form(""),
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_admin),
) -> HTMLResponse:
    """Process the new user form submission."""
    if not check_csrf_form(request, csrf_token):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="CSRF token missing or invalid")

    errors: list[str] = []

    # Validate username
    username = username.strip()
    if not _USERNAME_RE.match(username):
        errors.append(
            "Username must be 3-32 characters, using only letters, digits, and underscores."
        )

    # Check for duplicate username
    if username and get_user_by_username(username) is not None:
        errors.append(f"Username {username!r} already exists.")

    # Validate / generate password
    generated_password: str | None = None
    if password.strip():
        if len(password) < _MIN_PASSWORD_LEN:
            errors.append(f"Password must be at least {_MIN_PASSWORD_LEN} characters.")
    else:
        # Auto-generate a 16-char alphanumeric password
        generated_password = auth.generate_admin_password()
        password = generated_password

    if errors:
        return templates.TemplateResponse(
            request,
            "users/new.html",
            {
                "request": request,
                "error": " ".join(errors),
                "username": username,
                "is_admin": is_admin == "on",
                "generated_password": None,
            },
        )

    # Hash and create user
    pw_hash = auth.hash_password(password)
    admin_flag = is_admin == "on"
    new = create_user(username=username, password_hash=pw_hash, is_admin=admin_flag)

    return templates.TemplateResponse(
        request,
        "users/new.html",
        {
            "request": request,
            "error": None,
            "username": new.username,
            "is_admin": admin_flag,
            "generated_password": generated_password or password,
        },
    )


@router.get("/{user_id}/reset-password", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def reset_password_form(
    request: Request,
    user_id: int,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_admin),
) -> HTMLResponse:
    """Render the reset password confirmation form."""
    target = get_user_by_id(user_id)
    if target is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not found")

    return templates.TemplateResponse(
        request,
        "users/reset_password.html",
        {
            "request": request,
            "target_user": target,
            "new_password": None,
        },
    )


@router.post("/{user_id}/reset-password", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def reset_password_submit(
    request: Request,
    user_id: int,
    csrf_token: str = Form(""),
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_admin),
) -> HTMLResponse:
    """Generate a new password for the target user and display it once."""
    if not check_csrf_form(request, csrf_token):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="CSRF token missing or invalid")

    target = get_user_by_id(user_id)
    if target is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not found")

    new_password = auth.generate_admin_password()
    pw_hash = auth.hash_password(new_password)
    update_user_password(user_id, pw_hash)

    # Re-fetch to get updated data
    target = get_user_by_id(user_id)

    return templates.TemplateResponse(
        request,
        "users/reset_password.html",
        {
            "request": request,
            "target_user": target,
            "new_password": new_password,
        },
    )


@router.post("/{user_id}/delete")
async def delete_user_route(
    request: Request,
    user_id: int,
    csrf_token: str = Form(""),
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_admin),
) -> Response:
    """Delete a user. Refuses to delete the logged-in admin."""
    if not check_csrf_form(request, csrf_token):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="CSRF token missing or invalid")

    htmx = is_htmx(request)

    # Refuse to delete self
    if user_id == user.user_id:
        message = "You cannot delete your own account."
        if htmx:
            return _users_table(request, user, error=message)
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=message)

    target = get_user_by_id(user_id)
    if target is None or not delete_user(user_id):
        if htmx:
            return _users_table(request, user, error="User not found.")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not found")

    message = f"User {target.username} deleted."
    if htmx:
        return _users_table(request, user, notice=message)
    response = RedirectResponse(url="/users", status_code=303)
    set_flash(response, message, "success")
    return response


@router.post("/{user_id}/toggle-admin")
async def toggle_admin_route(
    request: Request,
    user_id: int,
    csrf_token: str = Form(""),
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_admin),
) -> Response:
    """Toggle a user's admin status. Refuses to demote self."""
    if not check_csrf_form(request, csrf_token):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="CSRF token missing or invalid")

    htmx = is_htmx(request)

    # Refuse to demote self
    if user_id == user.user_id:
        message = "You cannot change your own admin status."
        if htmx:
            return _users_table(request, user, error=message)
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=message)

    target = get_user_by_id(user_id)
    if target is None:
        if htmx:
            return _users_table(request, user, error="User not found.")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not found")

    new_admin = target.role != "admin"
    set_user_admin(user_id, new_admin)

    if new_admin:
        message = f"{target.username} is now an admin."
    else:
        message = f"{target.username} is no longer an admin."
    if htmx:
        return _users_table(request, user, notice=message)
    response = RedirectResponse(url="/users", status_code=303)
    set_flash(response, message, "success")
    return response
```

The panel sits under the create form, so the new token appears next to the button that made it. `hx-on::after-request` clears the form after a 2xx answer (htmx 2.0.4 supports the `hx-on::` shorthand; `event.detail.successful` is its 2xx flag). `maxlength="64"` matches `ApiToken.name` (`String(64)`, `db/models.py:65`).
```html
{% extends "base.html" %}

{% block title %}API Tokens{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<article>
  <h1>API Tokens</h1>

  <h2>Create New Token</h2>
  <form method="POST" action="/api-tokens"
        hx-post="/api-tokens"
        hx-target="#tokens-panel"
        hx-swap="outerHTML"
        hx-on::after-request="if (event.detail.successful) this.reset()">
    <input type="hidden" name="csrf_token" value="{{ request.state.csrf_token if request.state.csrf_token else '' }}">
    <div class="grid">
      <label>
        Token name
        <input type="text" name="name" placeholder="e.g. ci-deploy" maxlength="64" required>
      </label>
      <label>
        Expires in (days)
        <input type="number" name="expires_in_days" value="365" min="1" required>
      </label>
    </div>
    <button type="submit">Create Token</button>
  </form>

  {% include "partials/tokens_table.html" %}
</article>
{% endblock %}
```
