```html
{# PTZ pad for one camera. Context: camera, xaddr, ptz_enabled, duration_ms, duration_choices, ptz_message, ptz_error. #}
{% set csrf = request.state.csrf_token if request.state.csrf_token else '' %}
{% set cam_url = camera.name|urlencode %}
<article id="onvif-ptz">
  <header><strong>PTZ: {{ camera.name }}</strong></header>
  {% if xaddr %}<p class="small">ONVIF device service <code>{{ xaddr }}</code></p>{% endif %}
  {% if ptz_message %}<p class="flash flash-success" role="status">{{ ptz_message }}</p>{% endif %}
  {% if ptz_error %}<p class="flash flash-error" role="alert">{{ ptz_error }}</p>{% endif %}
  {% if ptz_enabled %}
  <form method="post" action="/onvif/cameras/{{ cam_url }}/ptz"
        hx-post="/onvif/cameras/{{ cam_url }}/ptz" hx-target="#onvif-ptz" hx-swap="outerHTML">
    <input type="hidden" name="csrf_token" value="{{ csrf }}">
    <label for="ptz-duration">Move for
      <select id="ptz-duration" name="duration_ms">
        {% for d in duration_choices %}
        <option value="{{ d }}"{% if d == duration_ms %} selected{% endif %}>{{ "until Stop" if d == 0 else ("%g s"|format(d / 1000)) }}</option>
        {% endfor %}
      </select>
    </label>
    <div class="ptz-pad">
      <span></span>
      <button type="submit" name="direction" value="up">Up</button>
      <span></span>
      <button type="submit" name="direction" value="left">Left</button>
      <button type="submit" name="direction" value="stop" class="secondary">Stop</button>
      <button type="submit" name="direction" value="right">Right</button>
      <button type="submit" name="direction" value="zoom_out" class="outline">Zoom out</button>
      <button type="submit" name="direction" value="down">Down</button>
      <button type="submit" name="direction" value="zoom_in" class="outline">Zoom in</button>
    </div>
  </form>
  {% else %}
  <p class="small">PTZ is off. Set <code>onvif.ptz_enabled: true</code> in config.yaml to use it.</p>
  {% endif %}
</article>
```

The pad is one form with named submit buttons; htmx 2.0.4 sends the clicked button's `name=value`
(`lastButtonClicked`), and a plain form post does the same, so `direction` always arrives.

Create `src/rtsp_warden/web/templates/partials/onvif_presets.html`:

```html
{# Presets panel for one camera. Context: camera, presets, ptz_enabled, preset_message, preset_error, preset_form. #}
{% set csrf = request.state.csrf_token if request.state.csrf_token else '' %}
{% set cam_url = camera.name|urlencode %}
<article id="onvif-presets">
  <header><strong>Presets</strong></header>
  {% if preset_message %}<p class="flash flash-success" role="status">{{ preset_message }}</p>{% endif %}
  {% if preset_error %}<p class="flash flash-error" role="alert">{{ preset_error }}</p>{% endif %}
  {% if presets %}
  <div class="table-wrap">
    <table>
      <thead>
        <tr><th>Name</th><th>Pan</th><th>Tilt</th><th>Zoom</th><th></th></tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {% for p in presets %}
        <tr>
          <td>{{ p.name }}</td>
          <td>{{ "%.2f"|format(p.pan) }}</td>
          <td>{{ "%.2f"|format(p.tilt) }}</td>
          <td>{{ "%.2f"|format(p.zoom) }}</td>
          <td>
            {% if ptz_enabled %}
            <form class="onvif-row-form" method="post" action="/onvif/cameras/{{ cam_url }}/ptz/goto"
                  hx-post="/onvif/cameras/{{ cam_url }}/ptz/goto" hx-target="#onvif-presets" hx-swap="outerHTML">
              <input type="hidden" name="csrf_token" value="{{ csrf }}">
              <input type="hidden" name="preset_name" value="{{ p.name }}">
              <button type="submit">Go</button>
            </form>
            {% endif %}
            <form class="onvif-row-form" method="post" action="/onvif/cameras/{{ cam_url }}/ptz/delete"
                  hx-post="/onvif/cameras/{{ cam_url }}/ptz/delete" hx-target="#onvif-presets" hx-swap="outerHTML"
                  hx-confirm="Delete preset {{ p.name }}?">
              <input type="hidden" name="csrf_token" value="{{ csrf }}">
              <input type="hidden" name="preset_name" value="{{ p.name }}">
              <button type="submit" class="secondary outline">Delete</button>
            </form>
          </td>
        </tr>
        {% endfor %}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  </div>
  {% else %}
  <p class="small">No presets saved for this camera.</p>
  {% endif %}
  <details{% if preset_form %} open{% endif %}>
    <summary>Add preset</summary>
    <form method="post" action="/onvif/cameras/{{ cam_url }}/ptz/save"
          hx-post="/onvif/cameras/{{ cam_url }}/ptz/save" hx-target="#onvif-presets" hx-swap="outerHTML">
      <input type="hidden" name="csrf_token" value="{{ csrf }}">
      <label for="preset-name">Name
        <input id="preset-name" name="preset_name" type="text" required maxlength="64"
               placeholder="front_gate" value="{{ preset_form.preset_name if preset_form else '' }}">
      </label>
      <div class="grid">
        <label for="preset-pan">Pan (-1 to 1)
          <input id="preset-pan" name="pan" type="number" step="0.01" min="-1" max="1"
                 value="{{ preset_form.pan if preset_form else '0' }}">
        </label>
        <label for="preset-tilt">Tilt (-1 to 1)
          <input id="preset-tilt" name="tilt" type="number" step="0.01" min="-1" max="1"
                 value="{{ preset_form.tilt if preset_form else '0' }}">
        </label>
        <label for="preset-zoom">Zoom (0 to 1)
          <input id="preset-zoom" name="zoom" type="number" step="0.01" min="0" max="1"
                 value="{{ preset_form.zoom if preset_form else '0' }}">
        </label>
      </div>
      <button type="submit">Save preset</button>
    </form>
    <p class="small">Values are typed in, not read from the camera. Saving a name that exists replaces it. Presets are written to config.yaml.</p>
  </details>
</article>
```

"Save current position" is relabelled "Add preset": there is no ONVIF GetStatus call, so the values are typed in.

- [ ] **Step 13: Swap 4xx HTML fragments in htmx and style the pad**

`src/rtsp_warden/web/static/js/warden.js` ends at line 10 with:

```js
document.addEventListener("htmx:configRequest", function (evt) {
  var meta = document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]');
  if (meta && meta.content) {
    evt.detail.headers["X-CSRF-Token"] = meta.content;
  }
});
```

Append after it, with one blank line in between (keep the existing listener as it is):

```js
// htmx 2 does not swap 4xx responses. Routes that answer a bad form post (422)
// or a missing camera or preset (404) send an HTML fragment meant for the page,
// so swap 4xx responses whose body is HTML. JSON errors (CSRF, auth, FastAPI
// validation) are still not swapped.
document.addEventListener("htmx:beforeSwap", function (evt) {
  var xhr = evt.detail.xhr;
  if (!xhr || xhr.status < 400 || xhr.status >= 500) {
    return;
  }
  var contentType = xhr.getResponseHeader("Content-Type") || "";
  if (contentType.indexOf("text/html") === 0) {
    evt.detail.shouldSwap = true;
    evt.detail.isError = false;
  }
});
```

Tasks 2 and 7 answer htmx requests with 200 and put errors inside the fragment, so this listener changes nothing
for them; it makes this task's 422 and 404 fragments visible (the skeleton pins 422 for bad input), and any later
route that answers htmx with a 4xx HTML fragment.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests for the init-config template**

Create `/home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2/tests/test_init_config_template.py`:

```python
"""`rtsp-warden init-config` template: env-based credentials, optional sub stream."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest
import yaml
from typer.testing import CliRunner

from rtsp_warden.cli import SAMPLE_CONFIG_YAML, app
from rtsp_warden.config import load_config

MAIN_URL = "rtsp://${CAM_USER}:${CAM_PASS}@192.168.1.50:554/Streaming/Channels/101"
COMMENTED_SUB_URL = (
    "# sub_url: rtsp://${CAM_USER}:${CAM_PASS}@192.168.1.50:554/Streaming/Channels/102"
)


def _isolate_env(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, *names: str) -> None:
    """Unset each name and make monkeypatch remove it again after the test.

    setenv first so monkeypatch records the original state; `_load_dotenv` writes
    os.environ directly and would otherwise leak into later tests.
    """
    for name in names:
        monkeypatch.setenv(name, "placeholder")
        monkeypatch.delenv(name)


def _write_template(out: Path) -> Path:
    result = CliRunner().invoke(app, ["init-config", "--out", str(out)])
    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
    return out


def test_template_has_no_inline_credentials() -> None:
    assert "user:pass" not in SAMPLE_CONFIG_YAML
    raw = yaml.safe_load(SAMPLE_CONFIG_YAML)
    assert raw["cameras"][0]["main_url"] == MAIN_URL


def test_template_sub_url_is_commented_out() -> None:
    raw = yaml.safe_load(SAMPLE_CONFIG_YAML)
    assert "sub_url" not in raw["cameras"][0]
    assert COMMENTED_SUB_URL in SAMPLE_CONFIG_YAML


def test_template_header_explains_the_env_file() -> None:
    header = SAMPLE_CONFIG_YAML.split("cameras:", 1)[0]
    assert ".env" in header
    assert "CAM_USER=" in header
    assert "CAM_PASS=" in header
    assert "Percent-encode" in header


def test_written_template_validates_with_env_set(
    tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setenv("CAM_USER", "u")
    monkeypatch.setenv("CAM_PASS", "p")
    cfg = load_config(_write_template(tmp_path / "config.yaml"))
    cam = cfg.cameras[0]
    assert cam.name == "front"
    assert cam.main_url == "rtsp://u:p@192.168.1.50:554/Streaming/Channels/101"
    assert cam.sub_url is None
    # No sub_url: the proxy (and the frame tap) fall back to the main stream.
    assert cam.proxy.stream == "main"


def test_written_template_without_env_names_the_missing_variable(
    tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    _isolate_env(monkeypatch, "CAM_USER", "CAM_PASS")
    out = _write_template(tmp_path / "config.yaml")
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="CAM_USER"):
        load_config(out)


def test_doctor_reads_env_file_next_to_generated_config(
    tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    """README quick start: init-config, a .env beside it, doctor run from elsewhere."""
    _isolate_env(monkeypatch, "CAM_USER", "CAM_PASS")
    run_dir = tmp_path / "run"
    run_dir.mkdir()
    monkeypatch.chdir(run_dir)
    out = _write_template(tmp_path / "etc" / "config.yaml")
    (out.parent / ".env").write_text('CAM_USER="u"\nCAM_PASS="p"\n', encoding="utf-8")

    result = CliRunner().invoke(app, ["doctor", "-c", str(out)])

    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
    assert "config loads" in result.output
    assert "CAM_USER" not in result.output
```

Notes for the engineer: `doctor` prints `FAIL` rows when ffmpeg is missing and still exits 0, and its proxy-port
row is `OK` or `IN USE` depending on the machine; neither affects these asserts. The values `u` / `p` are fake.

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the failing tests for the deployment files**

Create `/home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2/tests/test_deploy_writable.py`:

```python
"""Shipped deployments let the web UI write config.yaml and .env (ruling R9)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import yaml

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
UNIT = REPO / "packaging" / "systemd" / "rtsp-warden.service"
INSTALL_SH = REPO / "packaging" / "systemd" / "install.sh"


def _unit_paths(directive: str) -> list[str]:
    """Every path listed by `directive=` lines of the systemd unit."""
    paths: list[str] = []
    for line in UNIT.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        if line.startswith(f"{directive}="):
            paths.extend(line.split("=", 1)[1].split())
    return paths


def test_compose_mounts_config_dir_read_write() -> None:
    compose = yaml.safe_load((REPO / "docker-compose.yml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    volumes = compose["services"]["warden"]["volumes"]
    config_mounts = [v for v in volumes if isinstance(v, str) and v.split(":")[1] == "/app/config"]
    assert config_mounts == ["./config:/app/config"]


def test_systemd_unit_lets_the_service_write_its_config_dir() -> None:
    read_write = _unit_paths("ReadWritePaths")
    assert "/etc/rtsp-warden" in read_write
    assert "/etc/rtsp-warden" not in _unit_paths("ReadOnlyPaths")
    assert {"/var/lib/rtsp-warden", "/var/log/rtsp-warden"} <= set(read_write)


def test_install_script_makes_config_dir_group_writable() -> None:
    lines = INSTALL_SH.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    config_dir_lines = [
        line
        for line in lines
        if line.startswith("install -d ") and line.endswith(" /etc/rtsp-warden")
    ]
    assert config_dir_lines == ['install -d -m 0770 -o root -g "${SERVICE_GROUP}" /etc/rtsp-warden']
```

Why `install.sh` is in scope: `ReadWritePaths` only lifts systemd's read-only mount. `/etc/rtsp-warden` is
`0755 root:rtsp-warden` today, so the service user still could not create `config.yaml.tmp`, `.config.yaml.lock`
or `.env` there (`web/config_lock.py:26-33` creates the lock and temp file next to `config.yaml`).

```ini

```

with (one line; the `ReadOnlyPaths` line is deleted, not kept next to the new entry):

`packaging/systemd/install.sh`, replace lines 21-22:


with:

```bash
# Group-writable: the service saves config.yaml and .env here from the web UI.
install -d -m 0770 -o root -g "${SERVICE_GROUP}" /etc/rtsp-warden
echo "    /etc/rtsp-warden  (mode 770, root:${SERVICE_GROUP})"
```

(GNU `install -d` also applies `-m` to a directory that already exists, so re-running the script fixes old
installs; checked with coreutils 9.11.)

````markdown
When using **named volumes** or **freshly-created bind mounts**, the nonroot user can write to them. When using bind mounts that already contain files (e.g., recovered from a backup), the host directory must be readable AND writable by uid 65532.

```bash
# Named volumes: works out of the box
docker volume create warden-recordings
docker run -v warden-recordings:/app/recordings rtsp-warden:distroless

# Bind mounts: chown the host directory first
sudo chown -R 65532:65532 ./recordings
docker run -v $(pwd)/recordings:/app/recordings rtsp-warden:distroless
```
````

with:

````markdown
The app writes to all three mounts: `config/` (the web UI saves `config.yaml`, its `.config.yaml.lock` lock file, and the `.env` that holds the logins of cameras added from the UI), `recordings/` and `data/`. A bind mount keeps the owner of the host directory, so give the directories to the container user before the first start:

```bash
mkdir -p config recordings data
sudo chown -R 65532:65532 config recordings data   # distroless image (default): user nonroot
# sudo chown -R 1000:1000 config recordings data   # slim image: user warden
```

Edit `config/config.yaml` with `sudo` afterwards. Do not mount `config/` read-only (`:ro`): adding, editing or deleting a camera from the web UI then fails with an error naming the file.
````

12c. Insert the following block directly above line 88 (`## How it fits together`), after the blank line 87, so
one blank line separates it from line 86 (`The distroless image is 685 MB. ...`) and the block ends with a blank
line before `## How it fits together`. Do not edit line 86 or the Option B block (RW-3 owns the Docker quick start).

````markdown
## Adding a camera

Admins add, edit and delete cameras in the web UI, without a restart. Open **Cameras**, then **Add camera** (`/cameras/new`).

| Field | Notes |
|---|---|
| Name | 1 to 32 letters, digits, `-` or `_`, starting with a letter or digit. Must be unique; names that differ only in upper and lower case, or in `-` versus `_`, count as the same name. `new` is reserved. It cannot be changed later: to rename a camera, delete it and add it again. |
| Host | The camera's IP address or host name. |
| Username, password | The camera's login. They are not written to `config.yaml` (see below). |
| Main stream URL | Optional when **Fill from ONVIF** finds it. Left blank, it becomes `rtsp://<host>:554/`. |
| Sub stream URL | Optional. Without it, the live view and detection use the main stream, and only the main stream is recorded. |
| ONVIF port | Set by **Fill from ONVIF**; the PTZ and event pages use it (default 80). |
| Record | Record this camera to disk. |

**Fill from ONVIF** asks the camera for its stream URLs (`GetStreamUri`, WS-UsernameToken login) on ports 80, 8080, 888 and 2020, waiting at most 3 seconds per port, and uses the first port that answers. The URLs it returns are rewritten to the host you typed, because cameras often report an address you cannot reach. A wrong username or password stops at the first port that answers and says so.

**Test connection** runs `ffprobe` against the stream with a timeout and shows the codec, resolution, frame rate and one snapshot. It uses `runtime.ffprobe_path` when that is set, otherwise the `ffprobe` next to `runtime.ffmpeg_path`; both Docker images include it. Saving does not require a passing test.

**Save** adds the camera to `config.yaml` and starts it. Its MJPEG proxy port is the lowest free port from 9001 up that no other camera uses; the edit page shows it read-only.

### Where the credentials go

The form writes environment references into `config.yaml`, never the login itself:

```yaml
cameras:
  - name: front-door
    main_url: rtsp://${CAM_FRONT_DOOR_USER}:${CAM_FRONT_DOOR_PASS}@192.168.1.60:554/stream1
    onvif_port: 8080
    record:
      enabled: true
    proxy:
      enabled: true
      mode: mjpeg
      stream: main
      port: 9002
```

The variable names are `CAM_`, the camera name upper-cased with `-` turned into `_`, then `_USER` or `_PASS`. Their values are percent-encoded (so a password with `@`, `/`, `#` or `%` works) and saved in the `.env` file next to `config.yaml`, created with mode 0600. `serve`, `doctor` and `status` read that file, then `./.env`, at start; a variable that is already set in the environment (compose `environment:`, systemd `EnvironmentFile=`) wins. The repository's `.gitignore` ignores every `.env`.

For a camera you write by hand, use the same pattern: `rtsp://${CAM_USER}:${CAM_PASS}@host:554/path`, with `CAM_USER` and `CAM_PASS` in that `.env` and `@ : / ? # %` percent-encoded in the values.

### Editing and deleting

The camera page has **Edit** and **Delete** buttons. **Edit** (`/cameras/{name}/edit`; the old `/cameras/{name}/settings` page redirects there) changes the main and sub stream URLs (a blank sub URL removes it), the login (blank fields keep the current one), recording on or off, and the ONVIF port. Changing a URL or the recording switch restarts only that camera's ffmpeg. **Delete** removes the camera from `config.yaml` and stops it; its recordings and events are kept.

### The config directory must be writable

A save writes `config.yaml`, a `.config.yaml.lock` lock file, a temporary `config.yaml.tmp` and `.env` in the directory that holds `config.yaml`, so that directory must be writable by the user rtsp-warden runs as. The web UI rewrites `config.yaml` with PyYAML, so comments in it are not kept. When the directory is read-only, the page shows an error naming the file instead of saving.

- **Docker:** `docker-compose.yml` mounts `./config` read-write. Give the directory to the container user before the first start: `sudo chown -R 65532:65532 config` for the default distroless image (uid 1000 for the slim image). See [docker/README.md](docker/README.md#mounting-volumes).
- **systemd:** the unit lists `/etc/rtsp-warden` in `ReadWritePaths`, and `packaging/systemd/install.sh` creates it as `0770 root:rtsp-warden`. Logins of cameras added from the web UI land in `/etc/rtsp-warden/.env`; `warden.env` stays the place for everything else.

````

The example IP `192.168.1.60`, path `/stream1` and ports are illustrative; never put the owner's camera address
or any real login in the README.

```markdown
**Camera add, edit and delete** (`web/routes/camera_edit.py`) never write the in-memory,

`_locked_write_yaml`). Credentials are stored as `${CAM_<SLUG>_USER}` / `${CAM_<SLUG>_PASS}`
references (`env_slug`: upper-case, `-` to `_`); the percent-encoded values go to
`<config dir>/.env` through `web/env_file.upsert_env_vars` (mode 0600, locked) and into
`os.environ`. The running camera changes through `AppRuntime.request_add_camera` /
`request_restart_camera` / `request_remove_camera`, which the supervisor applies on the main
thread; the handlers are plain `def` so their blocking `.result(timeout=...)` waits run in the
threadpool. "Test connection" is `probe.probe_stream` (ffprobe plus one ffmpeg snapshot through
`subprocess.run`) and "Fill from ONVIF" is `onvif/media.discover_stream_uris`. A failed write
(`OSError`) becomes an error message naming the path, never a 500.
```

### Task 11: Mobile-width pass and markup checks for every RW-2 page

**Why.** Spec A.2 asks for a "mobile-width layout check for every page". There is no browser harness in this
project (ui-shell map G22), so the check has two halves: a TestClient test that pins the markup and CSS rules a
375 px phone needs, and one measured pass in the Browser pane. Measured while planning (pages rendered through
TestClient, served statically, `document.documentElement.scrollWidth` read in a 375 px frame):

- master: `/users` 617 px, `/api-tokens` 510 px, `/cameras/{name}/zones` 451 px wide (tables sit directly in the
  page). Tasks 1, 2, 3 and 9 already wrap their own tables; `cameras/zones.html`, `settings/form.html` and
  `partials/health_status.html` are touched by no earlier task and still carry bare tables.
- with Task 1's CSS and Task 3's card and page-header markup: a valid 31-character camera name without hyphens
  (`front_door_camera_number_one_xx`; names match `^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,31}$`) makes the page 576 px wide.
  Neither the page-header `<h1>` nor the card header link nor a long redacted URL in a `<code>` has a break
  opportunity. `overflow-wrap: anywhere` on those four selectors brings it back to 375 px (also at 320 px).
- after this task every RW-2 page measured 375 px.

The rules the test pins:

1. Every `<table>` sits inside an element with class `table-wrap` (Task 1: `overflow-x: auto`), so a wide table
   scrolls inside its card instead of dragging the page sideways.
2. No inline `style` sets `width` / `min-width` wider than a phone (a percentage or `vw` above 100, or a fixed
   length above 320 px), and no inline `grid-template-columns` overrides Pico's one-column `.grid` (ui-shell map
   Q5 items 10, 11, 14).
3. Every full page extends `base.html`, so it carries `<meta name="viewport">`.
4. `warden.css` keeps Task 1's phone rules (`.table-wrap`, a 600 px block that stacks `.grid`, the
   `minmax(min(300px, 100%), 1fr)` camera grid) and gains this task's long-word and zone-placeholder rules.

The pass also found a page that was broken outright, not only narrow: the zones page opens the zone editor with
a plain link (`cameras/zones.html:25-26`, `:46`), but `GET /cameras/{name}/zones/editor` returns the bare
`cameras/zones_editor.html` fragment (`routes/zones.py:53-111`; the template has no `{% extends %}`). The browser
shows it with no stylesheet, no Alpine and no htmx, so the grid never renders, nothing is clickable, and without a
viewport tag a phone lays it out 980 px wide. The fix serves the editor inside the layout for a normal request and
keeps the fragment for htmx. The editor's "no snapshot" placeholder also binds a fixed size
(`zones_editor.html:57`, `:style="'width:' + (frameWidth > 800 ? 800 : frameWidth) + 'px; ...'"`), 800 px on a
375 px screen; Alpine's string `:style` replaces the whole `style` attribute (vendored `alpine.min.js`:
`setAttribute("style", t)`), so today the placeholder also loses its static background. It becomes a CSS class plus
an object binding that keeps the frame's aspect ratio.

**Starting point.** This task runs after Tasks 1-10 and consumes what they ship instead of repeating it. Task 1
(Step 21) changes the `.camera-grid` minimum to `minmax(min(300px, 100%), 1fr)` and appends `.table-wrap`,
`.site-nav` wrapping, `.page-header`, `.sr-only`, `.small` and a `@media (max-width: 600px)` block with
`.grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr !important; }` and tighter table cells; it also creates
`partials/page_header.html` (`title`, `subtitle`, `header_actions`). Task 2 wraps the users and tokens tables in
`partials/users_table.html` / `partials/tokens_table.html`, drops the inline grids of `users/list.html:32`,
`users/new.html:34`, `tokens/list.html:30`, and adds `.row-actions` and `_common.is_htmx`. Task 3 replaces
`cameras/detail.html` whole (its three tables in `.table-wrap`, the "Recent recordings" table gone) and deletes
`cameras/settings.html`. Task 7's `cameras/form.html`, `partials/probe_result.html`, `partials/onvif_fill.html` have
no table. Task 9 replaces the ONVIF page and wraps the tables of `partials/onvif_presets.html`,
`onvif_subscriptions.html`, `onvif_discover.html`. Step 11 is the safety net for anything that drifted from
those plans; the test decides, nothing is wrapped twice.

**Ownership.** Every file touched is on the RW-2 list in decisions.md: `warden.css`, `cameras/zones.html`,

Create `/home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2/tests/test_mobile_markup.py`:
```python
"""Mobile-width markup checks for every RW-2 page (RW-2 Task 11).

This project has no browser harness, so a 375 px phone is approximated by the
markup rules that keep the page itself from scrolling sideways:

* every ``<table>`` sits inside an element with class ``table-wrap``
  (``overflow-x: auto`` in warden.css), so a wide table scrolls inside its card;
* no inline ``style`` sets ``width`` / ``min-width`` wider than a phone (a
  percentage or ``vw`` above 100, or a fixed length above 320 px), and no inline
  ``grid-template-columns`` overrides Pico's one-column ``.grid`` below 768 px;
* every full page extends ``base.html``, so it carries the viewport meta tag.

Rendered pages are checked through TestClient. Template sources are checked as
well, because some RW-2 fragments only reach the browser through htmx.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from html.parser import HTMLParser
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
import yaml
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from rtsp_warden import auth
from rtsp_warden.config import load_config
from rtsp_warden.db.schema import get_user_by_username
from rtsp_warden.web.app import create_app
from rtsp_warden.web.config import WebSettings
from rtsp_warden.web.paths import STATIC_DIR, TEMPLATES_DIR

CAMERA = "front"

# 375 px minus Pico's two 1rem gutters is 343 px; 320 px leaves room for card padding.
MAX_FIXED_PX = 320.0

# Every RW-2 page as an admin sees it. The tokens page lives at /api-tokens.
ADMIN_PAGES = (
    "/",
    "/cameras",
    f"/cameras/{CAMERA}",
    "/cameras/new",
    f"/cameras/{CAMERA}/edit",
    f"/cameras/{CAMERA}/zones",
    f"/cameras/{CAMERA}/zones/editor",
    "/health",
    "/settings",
    "/users",
    "/users/new",
    "/users/1/reset-password",
    "/api-tokens",
    "/onvif",
)

# Pages whose fixture data renders at least one table, so the wrap check bites.
PAGES_WITH_TABLES = (
    f"/cameras/{CAMERA}",
    f"/cameras/{CAMERA}/zones",
    "/health",
    "/settings",
    "/users",
    "/api-tokens",
)

# Templates RW-3 owns, plus dashboard.html, whose "Recent events" and "Recent
# recordings" blocks belong to RW-3; the dashboard is checked as a rendered page.
NOT_RW2_DIRS = ("actions/", "alerts/", "clips/", "events/", "recordings/")
NOT_RW2_FILES = frozenset(
    {
        "dashboard.html",
        "cameras/_detection_panel.html",
        "cameras/detection_classes.html",
        "cameras/sensitivity.html",
        "partials/_detection_badge.html",
        "partials/action_test_result.html",
        "partials/detector_list.html",
        "partials/event_card.html",
        "partials/event_row.html",
        "partials/hls_player.html",
        "partials/timeline.html",
        "partials/zone_kind.html",
    }
)

_VOID = frozenset(
    {
        "area",
        "base",
        "br",
        "col",
        "embed",
        "hr",
        "img",
        "input",
        "link",
        "meta",
        "param",
        "source",
        "track",
        "wbr",
    }
)
_JINJA = re.compile(r"\{#.*?#\}|\{%.*?%\}|\{\{.*?\}\}", re.DOTALL)
_WIDTH_DECL = re.compile(r"(?:^|;)\s*(min-width|width)\s*:\s*([^;]+)", re.IGNORECASE)
_GRID_COLUMNS = re.compile(r"(?:^|;)\s*grid-template-columns\s*:", re.IGNORECASE)
_LENGTH = re.compile(r"^(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)(px|rem|em|vw|%)$")
_MEDIA_600 = re.compile(r"@media\s*\(\s*max-width\s*:\s*600px\s*\)\s*\{")
_GRID_ONE_COLUMN = re.compile(
    r"\.grid(?![\w-])[^{}]*\{[^}]*grid-template-columns\s*:\s*1fr\s*(?:!important\s*)?[;}]"
)
_CSS_COMMENT = re.compile(r"/\*.*?\*/", re.DOTALL)
_CSS_RULE = re.compile(r"([^{}]+)\{([^{}]*)\}")


def style_problem(style: str) -> str | None:
    """Return why an inline style is too wide for a phone, or None when it fits."""
    if _GRID_COLUMNS.search(style):
        return "inline grid-template-columns stops Pico stacking the grid on phones"
    for prop, raw in _WIDTH_DECL.findall(style):
        value = raw.strip().lower()
        match = _LENGTH.match(value)
        if match is None:
            continue  # auto, fit-content, calc(), min(), var() are not judged here
        number, unit = float(match.group(1)), match.group(2)
        if unit in ("%", "vw"):
            if number > 100:
                return f"{prop}: {value} is wider than the screen"
        elif number * (16 if unit in ("rem", "em") else 1) > MAX_FIXED_PX:
            return f"{prop}: {value} is wider than a 375 px phone"
    return None


class MarkupScan(HTMLParser):
    """Collect mobile-width problems from one HTML document or template source."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(convert_charrefs=True)
        self._open: list[tuple[str, frozenset[str]]] = []
        self.tables = 0
        self.unwrapped_tables: list[str] = []
        self.wide_styles: list[str] = []
        self.has_viewport = False

    @property
    def problems(self) -> list[str]:
        return self.unwrapped_tables + self.wide_styles

    def handle_starttag(self, tag: str, attrs: list[tuple[str, str | None]]) -> None:
        values = {name: value or "" for name, value in attrs}
        line = self.getpos()[0]
        if tag == "table":
            self.tables += 1
            if not any("table-wrap" in classes for _, classes in self._open):
                self.unwrapped_tables.append(f"line {line}: <table> outside .table-wrap")
        problem = style_problem(values.get("style", ""))
        if problem:
            self.wide_styles.append(f'line {line}: <{tag} style="{values["style"]}">: {problem}')
        if tag == "meta" and values.get("name") == "viewport":
            self.has_viewport = True
        if tag not in _VOID:
            self._open.append((tag, frozenset(values.get("class", "").split())))

    def handle_endtag(self, tag: str) -> None:
        for index in range(len(self._open) - 1, -1, -1):
            if self._open[index][0] == tag:
                del self._open[index:]
                return


def scan(html: str) -> MarkupScan:
    """Parse *html* and return the collected problems."""
    parser = MarkupScan()
    parser.feed(html)
    parser.close()
    return parser


def _strip_jinja(source: str) -> str:
    """Drop Jinja tags but keep their newlines, so reported line numbers match the file."""
    return _JINJA.sub(lambda m: "\n" * m.group(0).count("\n"), source)


def _rw2_templates() -> list[Path]:
    found = []
    for path in sorted(TEMPLATES_DIR.rglob("*.html")):
        rel = path.relative_to(TEMPLATES_DIR).as_posix()
        if rel.startswith(NOT_RW2_DIRS) or rel in NOT_RW2_FILES:
            continue
        found.append(path)
    return found


def _media_600_bodies(css: str) -> list[str]:
    bodies = []
    for match in _MEDIA_600.finditer(css):
        depth, index = 1, match.end()
        while depth and index < len(css):
            if css[index] == "{":
                depth += 1
            elif css[index] == "}":
                depth -= 1
            index += 1
        bodies.append(css[match.end() : index - 1])
    return bodies


def _css_rules(css: str) -> list[tuple[set[str], str]]:
    """Return (selectors, body) for every innermost rule, comments removed."""
    return [
        ({part.strip() for part in selectors.split(",")}, body)
        for selectors, body in _CSS_RULE.findall(_CSS_COMMENT.sub("", css))
    ]


def _warden_css() -> str:
    return (STATIC_DIR / "css" / "warden.css").read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def _login(client: TestClient) -> None:
    client.get("/login")
    token = client.cookies.get("warden_csrf", "")
    r = client.post(
        "/login",
        data={"username": "admin", "password": "testpass123", "csrf_token": token},
        follow_redirects=False,
    )
    assert r.status_code == 303


@pytest.fixture
def config_file(tmp_path: Path) -> Path:
    recordings = str(tmp_path / "recordings")
    raw = {
        "cameras": [
            {
                "name": CAMERA,
                "main_url": "rtsp://u:p@h/m",
                "record": {"enabled": False, "output_dir": recordings},
                "proxy": {"enabled": True, "mode": "mjpeg", "stream": "main", "port": 9001},
                "zones": [
                    {
                        "name": "driveway",
                        "grid_cols": 16,
                        "grid_rows": 16,
                        "blocked_cells": [[0, 0], [1, 0]],
                        "frame_width": 1920,
                        "frame_height": 1080,
                    }
                ],
            },
            {
                "name": "back",
                "main_url": "rtsp://u:p@h/b",
                "record": {"enabled": False, "output_dir": recordings},
                "proxy": {"enabled": False},
            },
        ],
        "runtime": {"workspace_dir": str(tmp_path / "workspace")},
    }
    path = tmp_path / "config.yaml"
    path.write_text(yaml.safe_dump(raw, sort_keys=False), encoding="utf-8")
    return path


@pytest.fixture
def admin_client(
    db_with_user: str, config_file: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> TestClient:
    monkeypatch.setenv("WARDEN_AUTH_ENABLED", "true")
    cfg = load_config(config_file)
    app = create_app(WebSettings(), cfg=cfg, runtime_provider=lambda: None, config_path=config_file)
    client = TestClient(app)
    _login(client)
    admin = get_user_by_username("admin")
    assert admin is not None
    auth.create_api_token(admin, "phone-check")
    return client


def test_style_problem_rules() -> None:
    assert style_problem("") is None
    assert style_problem("display:inline") is None
    assert style_problem("display:block; max-width:100%;") is None
    assert style_problem("width:100%; height:80px") is None
    assert style_problem("width: 320px") is None
    assert style_problem("min-width: 20rem") is None
    assert style_problem("width:800px") == "width: 800px is wider than a 375 px phone"
    assert style_problem("min-width: 30rem") == "min-width: 30rem is wider than a 375 px phone"
    assert style_problem("width: 120%") == "width: 120% is wider than the screen"
    assert style_problem("grid-template-columns: 1fr auto;") is not None


def test_scan_finds_unwrapped_and_wrapped_tables() -> None:
    result = scan(
        '<div class="table-wrap"><table><tr><td>a</td></tr></table></div>'
        "<article><table><tr><td>b</td></tr></table></article>"
    )
    assert result.tables == 2
    assert result.unwrapped_tables == ["line 1: <table> outside .table-wrap"]


def test_strip_jinja_keeps_line_numbers() -> None:
    source = "{# a\nthree-line\ncomment #}\n<table></table>\n"
    assert scan(_strip_jinja(source)).unwrapped_tables == ["line 4: <table> outside .table-wrap"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("path", ADMIN_PAGES)
def test_admin_page_fits_a_phone(admin_client: TestClient, path: str) -> None:
    r = admin_client.get(path)
    assert r.status_code == 200, f"GET {path} returned {r.status_code}"
    result = scan(r.text)
    assert result.has_viewport, f"{path} does not extend base.html (no viewport meta tag)"
    assert result.problems == [], f"{path}: {result.problems}"


def test_login_page_fits_a_phone(admin_client: TestClient) -> None:
    anonymous = TestClient(admin_client.app)
    r = anonymous.get("/login")
    assert r.status_code == 200
    result = scan(r.text)
    assert result.has_viewport
    assert result.problems == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize("path", PAGES_WITH_TABLES)
def test_fixture_data_renders_tables(admin_client: TestClient, path: str) -> None:
    """Guard: these pages must render tables, or the wrap check proves nothing."""
    assert scan(admin_client.get(path).text).tables >= 1, path


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "template", _rw2_templates(), ids=lambda p: p.relative_to(TEMPLATES_DIR).as_posix()
)
def test_template_source_fits_a_phone(template: Path) -> None:
    result = scan(_strip_jinja(template.read_text(encoding="utf-8")))
    rel = template.relative_to(TEMPLATES_DIR).as_posix()
    assert result.problems == [], f"{rel}: {result.problems}"


def test_css_stacks_grids_and_scrolls_tables_on_phones() -> None:
    css = _warden_css()
    bodies = _media_600_bodies(css)
    assert bodies, "warden.css has no @media (max-width: 600px) block"
    assert any(_GRID_ONE_COLUMN.search(body) for body in bodies), (
        "no @media (max-width: 600px) block stacks .grid into one column"
    )
    assert re.search(r"\.table-wrap\s*\{[^}]*overflow-x\s*:\s*auto", css), "no .table-wrap rule"
    assert "minmax(min(300px, 100%), 1fr)" in css, ".camera-grid overflows a 320 px phone"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "selector", ["main h1", ".camera-card header a", "main code", ".camera-card-body small"]
)
def test_css_breaks_long_names_and_urls(selector: str) -> None:
    """A 32-character camera name with underscores or a long RTSP URL has no break point."""
    assert any(
        selector in selectors and re.search(r"overflow-wrap\s*:\s*anywhere", body)
        for selectors, body in _css_rules(_warden_css())
    ), f"{selector} does not break long words"


def test_css_caps_the_zone_placeholder_at_the_screen_width() -> None:
    bodies = [
        body for selectors, body in _css_rules(_warden_css()) if ".zone-placeholder" in selectors
    ]
    assert any(re.search(r"max-width\s*:\s*100%", body) for body in bodies), (
        ".zone-placeholder is not capped at max-width: 100%"
    )


def test_zone_editor_link_opens_a_full_page(admin_client: TestClient) -> None:
    r = admin_client.get(f"/cameras/{CAMERA}/zones/editor")
    assert r.status_code == 200
    assert scan(r.text).has_viewport, "the zone editor link opens a bare fragment"
    assert "/static/js/alpine.min.js" in r.text
    assert "zoneEditor(" in r.text


def test_zone_editor_htmx_request_gets_the_fragment(admin_client: TestClient) -> None:
    r = admin_client.get(f"/cameras/{CAMERA}/zones/editor", headers={"HX-Request": "true"})
    assert r.status_code == 200
    assert "<html" not in r.text
    assert "zoneEditor(" in r.text


def test_zone_editor_placeholder_has_no_fixed_pixel_size(admin_client: TestClient) -> None:
    html = admin_client.get("/cameras/back/zones/editor").text
    assert 'class="zone-placeholder"' in html
    assert ":style=\"{ aspectRatio: frameWidth + ' / ' + frameHeight }\"" in html
    assert "+ 'px" not in html
```

Notes for the reader: `db_with_user` (conftest) points `WARDEN_DB_URL` at a temp SQLite file with `monkeypatch` and
creates admin `admin` / `testpass123` (user id 1). `runtime_provider=lambda: None` makes every camera status
`unknown`, which is enough for markup. The API token exists so `/api-tokens` renders its table.
`TestClient(admin_client.app)` is a second client with an empty cookie jar, so `/login` renders instead of
redirecting. The template-source check strips Jinja tags but keeps their newlines, so a reported line number is the
line in the file. `_css_rules` reads only innermost rules, so a rule inside a `@media` block counts too.

- [ ] **Step 4: Append the mobile CSS section**

Append at the very end of
`/home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2/src/rtsp_warden/web/static/css/warden.css`
(after Task 9's ONVIF block), with one blank line before:
```css
/* --- Mobile width (RW-2 Task 11): no sideways page scroll on a 375 px phone --- */
main h1,
.camera-card header a,
main code,
.camera-card-body small {
  overflow-wrap: anywhere;
}
.zone-placeholder {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  width: 800px;
  max-width: 100%;
  background: #222;
  color: #999;
}
@media (max-width: 600px) {
  .camera-card header {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 0.25rem;
  }
}
```
What each rule is for. `overflow-wrap: anywhere` lets a camera name without hyphens break inside the page-header
`<h1>` and the card header link, and a long redacted RTSP URL break inside a `<code>` (detail page cells, probe
result) or the card's URL line; unlike `break-word` it also lowers the element's minimum width, so a flex item
(Task 1's `.page-header` hgroup, Task 3's card header) can shrink instead of pushing the page wider. Ordinary text
still wraps at spaces. `.zone-placeholder` replaces the editor placeholder's inline style and 800 px binding
(Step 7): 800 px on a desktop, never wider than its column. The 600 px block lets the card header put the status
badge on a second line. Task 1's 600 px block already tightens table cells and stacks `.grid`; nothing here repeats
it. RW-3 later appends its own section after this one (decisions.md: append-only under
`/* --- detection (RW-3) --- */`).

Create `/home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2/src/rtsp_warden/web/templates/cameras/zones_editor_page.html`:
```html
{% extends "base.html" %}
{% block title %}Zone editor: {{ camera_name }}{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
{% set title = "Detection zone editor" %}
{% set subtitle = camera_name %}
{% include "partials/page_header.html" %}

{% include "cameras/zones_editor.html" %}

<p><a href="/cameras/{{ camera_name }}/zones">&larr; Back to zones</a></p>
{% endblock %}
```
The include gets the route's whole context, so `zones_editor.html` renders exactly as it does as a fragment. Its
inline `<script>` that defines `zoneEditor` runs while the page is parsed, before the deferred Alpine starts, so
`x-data="zoneEditor({...})"` finds the function. The editor's save form (`hx-post=".../zones"`, `hx-target="body"`)
now runs with htmx loaded: the route's 303 is followed and the zones page replaces the body.

- [ ] **Step 7: Size the editor placeholder by the frame's aspect ratio**

In `/home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2/src/rtsp_warden/web/templates/cameras/zones_editor.html`,
replace lines 56-57:
```html
        <div style="background:#222; color:#999; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center;"
             :style="'width:' + (frameWidth > 800 ? 800 : frameWidth) + 'px; height:' + (frameHeight > 450 ? 450 : frameHeight) + 'px;'">
```
with:
```html
        <div class="zone-placeholder"
             :style="{ aspectRatio: frameWidth + ' / ' + frameHeight }">
```
Alpine's object form sets one property (`el.style.setProperty("aspect-ratio", ...)`) and keeps the class styles;
the string form replaced the whole `style` attribute. The placeholder is now as wide as its column allows (at most
800 px) with the frame's height ratio, and the absolutely positioned SVG grid (line 63, `width:100%;
height:100%`) covers it exactly, so `handleClick`'s screen-to-viewBox scaling stays right. Measured at 375 px:
placeholder 311 x 175 px for a 1920 x 1080 frame, SVG 311 x 175 px.

- [ ] **Step 14: Render every page for the 375 px pass**

Rendering through TestClient means the Browser pane never logs in (no password is typed into a browser) and no
camera or ffmpeg is involved. Create `/tmp/rw2-render-pages.py` (outside the repository):
```python
"""Render every RW-2 page to static HTML for the 375 px Browser-pane check (RW-2 Task 11).

Run from the worktree:  uv run python /tmp/rw2-render-pages.py
It writes /tmp/rw2-phone-check/site/<page>.html and a copy of web/static next to them.
Nothing is written inside the repository.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import shutil
from pathlib import Path

OUT = Path("/tmp/rw2-phone-check")
SITE = OUT / "site"
os.environ["WARDEN_DB_URL"] = f"sqlite:///{OUT / 'warden.db'}"
os.environ["WARDEN_AUTH_ENABLED"] = "true"

import yaml  # noqa: E402
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient  # noqa: E402

from rtsp_warden import auth  # noqa: E402
from rtsp_warden.config import load_config  # noqa: E402
from rtsp_warden.db.engine import reset_engine  # noqa: E402
from rtsp_warden.db.schema import (  # noqa: E402
    create_admin_user,
    create_user,
    ensure_schema,
    get_user_by_username,
)
from rtsp_warden.web.app import create_app  # noqa: E402
from rtsp_warden.web.config import WebSettings  # noqa: E402
from rtsp_warden.web.paths import STATIC_DIR  # noqa: E402

LONG = "a_long_camera_name_for_phones_01"
PAGES = {
    "dashboard": "/",
    "cameras": "/cameras",
    "detail": "/cameras/front",
    "detail_long": f"/cameras/{LONG}",
    "new": "/cameras/new",
    "edit": "/cameras/front/edit",
    "zones": "/cameras/front/zones",
    "editor": "/cameras/front/zones/editor",
    "health": "/health",
    "settings": "/settings",
    "users": "/users",
    "users_new": "/users/new",
    "reset": "/users/1/reset-password",
    "tokens": "/api-tokens",
    "onvif": "/onvif",
}


def main() -> None:
    shutil.rmtree(OUT, ignore_errors=True)
    SITE.mkdir(parents=True)
    rec = str(OUT / "rec")
    zone = {
        "name": "driveway",
        "grid_cols": 16,
        "grid_rows": 16,
        "blocked_cells": [[0, 0], [1, 0]],
        "frame_width": 1920,
        "frame_height": 1080,
    }
    raw = {
        "cameras": [
            {
                "name": "front",
                "main_url": "rtsp://u:p@h/m",
                "record": {"enabled": False, "output_dir": rec},
                "proxy": {"enabled": True, "mode": "mjpeg", "stream": "main", "port": 9001},
                "zones": [zone],
            },
            {
                "name": LONG,
                "main_url": "rtsp://u:p@192.168.100.200:554/unreachable/stream/path/that/is/long",
                "record": {"enabled": False, "output_dir": rec},
                "proxy": {"enabled": False},
            },
        ],
        "runtime": {"workspace_dir": str(OUT / "workspace")},
    }
    config_path = OUT / "config.yaml"
    config_path.write_text(yaml.safe_dump(raw, sort_keys=False), encoding="utf-8")

    reset_engine()
    ensure_schema()
    create_admin_user("admin", auth.hash_password("testpass123"))
    create_user("a_rather_long_username_32chars_x", auth.hash_password("x" * 16))
    admin = get_user_by_username("admin")
    assert admin is not None
    auth.create_api_token(admin, "phone-check")

    app = create_app(
        WebSettings(),
        cfg=load_config(config_path),
        runtime_provider=lambda: None,
        config_path=config_path,
    )
    anonymous = TestClient(app)
    (SITE / "login.html").write_text(anonymous.get("/login").text, encoding="utf-8")

    client = TestClient(app)
    client.get("/login")
    token = client.cookies.get("warden_csrf", "")
    r = client.post(
        "/login",
        data={"username": "admin", "password": "testpass123", "csrf_token": token},
        follow_redirects=False,
    )
    assert r.status_code == 303, r.status_code
    for name, path in PAGES.items():
        r = client.get(path)
        assert r.status_code == 200, f"GET {path} -> {r.status_code}"
        (SITE / f"{name}.html").write_text(r.text, encoding="utf-8")
    shutil.copytree(STATIC_DIR, SITE / "static")
    reset_engine()
    print(f"rendered {len(PAGES) + 1} pages into {SITE}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
`testpass123` is the test-fixture password of `tests/conftest.py`, used only in-process against this throwaway
database. `a_long_camera_name_for_phones_01` is a valid 32-character name with no hyphen, the worst case for the
page header and the card. Run:
```bash
uv run --directory /home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2 python /tmp/rw2-render-pages.py
```
Expected: alembic INFO lines, then `rendered 16 pages into /tmp/rw2-phone-check/site`.

Step 16 uses 8099):

```bash
python3 -m http.server 8097 --bind 127.0.0.1 --directory /tmp/rw2-phone-check/site
```

- [ ] **Step 15: Measure every page at 375 px in the Browser pane**

Open `http://127.0.0.1:8097/dashboard.html` in the Browser pane (`preview_start` with that `url`), set the mobile
preset (`resize_window`, `preset: "mobile"`, 375 x 812), then run this with `javascript_tool` (a person without the
Browser pane: Chrome DevTools device toolbar at 375 px, paste it into the console). It loads each page into a
375 px frame and reports the page's scroll width and the first elements that stick out, ignoring content inside a
`.table-wrap` (which scrolls by design):
```javascript
const pages = ["login", "dashboard", "cameras", "detail", "detail_long", "new", "edit", "zones",
  "editor", "health", "settings", "users", "users_new", "reset", "tokens", "onvif"];
const out = {};
for (const p of pages) {
  const f = document.createElement("iframe");
  f.style.cssText = "position:absolute;left:0;top:0;width:375px;height:812px;border:0;visibility:hidden";
  document.body.appendChild(f);
  await new Promise((res) => { f.onload = res; f.src = "/" + p + ".html"; });
  await new Promise((res) => setTimeout(res, 500));
  const d = f.contentDocument.documentElement;
  const offenders = [...f.contentDocument.querySelectorAll("body *")]
    .filter((e) => e.getBoundingClientRect().right > d.clientWidth + 1 && !e.closest(".table-wrap"))
    .slice(0, 5)
    .map((e) => e.tagName.toLowerCase() + (typeof e.className === "string" && e.className
      ? "." + e.className.trim().split(/\s+/).join(".") : ""));
  out[p] = d.scrollWidth > d.clientWidth ? { scrollWidth: d.scrollWidth, offenders } : "ok";
  f.remove();
}
out
```
Expected: every page `"ok"` (verified while planning on master plus this task's edits and Task 1's CSS and nav
class). Then screenshot `users.html`, `detail_long.html` and `editor.html` (`navigate`, then `computer`
`screenshot`): the users table scrolls inside its card, the long name and the long redacted URL wrap, the editor
shows a dark 16:9 placeholder with the grid over it.

If a page is not `"ok"`, its `offenders` name the element. Fix it in that template or in the Task 11 section of
`warden.css`, add the case to `tests/test_mobile_markup.py` when the checker can express it (a table, an inline
width, an inline grid, a CSS rule), rerun Steps 12, 14 and this step, and add one sentence naming the fix to the
commit message in Step 18. htmx polling requests in these static pages answer 404 (there is no app behind the
static server); that is expected and changes nothing on the page.

### Task 12: Manual verification against the Foscam (gated on the owner's OK; never a gate)

**Purpose.** Run the finished `feat/ui-pass` build once against the owner's real camera, end to end, the way a
home-lab user would: add it through the form (ONVIF fill, Test connection, Save with hot-add), watch the card go
to running, see a failure rendered with a redacted error, edit `record.enabled`, restart `serve`, and delete the
camera. Spec Appendix A.2 bullets 2-3; rulings R3, R9, R10, R22. Tasks 1-11 already pin every behaviour offline
with fakes. This task covers what fakes cannot: the Foscam's real ONVIF answers on port 888 (WS-UsernameToken
against the camera's own clock), ffprobe/ffmpeg against its real stream, the supervisor's hot-add, restart and
remove with a real ffmpeg process, and a check that no page, log or endpoint shows the real password.

**Never a gate.** The task runs only after the owner says yes in chat (Step 1). If the owner declines, the camera is
unreachable, or a step fails, nothing in RW-2 is blocked: record what happened and report it. This task changes no
code and adds no tests. A defect found here becomes a follow-up fix task (see "Defects"). It is not fixed inside
Task 12.

**Paths used in every command.** Shell state does not persist between Bash calls, so commands spell them out.
- Worktree (WT): `/home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2` (branch `feat/ui-pass`).
- Scratch directory: `/home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw2-foscam-scratch`. It sits
  outside both worktrees, inside the main checkout's `.worktrees/` directory, which RW-0 Task 0.1 made
  git-ignored. The config, the `.env` the app writes next to `config.yaml` (R3), the database, the logs and the
  recordings all live there. Nothing in it is ever added to git, and Step 23 deletes it.
- Web UI: `http://127.0.0.1:8098` (Task 11 used 8099; a different port avoids a leftover server). The camera's
  MJPEG side server gets port 9001 from `allocate_proxy_port` (Task 6).
- Camera: Foscam C1 V3 reached through the owner's port forward at `192.168.1.72` (RTSP 554, ONVIF 888, HTTP/CGI
  88). Known facts: RTSP `videoMain` is H.264 1280x720 at 15 fps; `videoSub` sends no video, so the camera is saved
  with `main_url` only; ONVIF needs WS-UsernameToken and reports the camera's LAN address `192.168.4.29`, which
  Task 5's `rewrite_host` points back at `192.168.1.72`.

**Credential rules (binding for this task).**
- The agent never types, pastes, prints, logs or commits the Foscam user name or password. They are a real device
  credential, not a test value. The owner types them into the add-camera form in the Browser pane (Step 8). The app
  then stores them only in the scratch `.env` (mode 0600) as `CAM_FOSCAM_C1_USER` / `CAM_FOSCAM_C1_PASS`
  (percent-encoded, Task 6).
- The app login is a throwaway test account created in Step 3 (`admin` plus a generated password in the scratch
  `admin.env`). The agent may type that password into the local login page. It never repeats it in chat.
- Never run commands that print secrets: `cat`/`less`/`grep` on `.env` (the test login in `admin.env` is read
  once, in Step 6), `env`, `printenv`, `ps aux`, `ps -ef`, `pgrep -a`, `pgrep -l -f`, reads of `/proc/*/cmdline`
  or `/proc/*/environ` (ffmpeg's argv holds the expanded URL with the password), or an unfiltered `cat`/`tail` of
  a serve log (`serve-1.log` also holds the test admin password, logged by `db/bootstrap.py:48`). Read logs only
  through the filter in Step 5 and processes only through the checker's `procs` command.
- Read page text through the masking snippets J3/J4 before quoting anything in chat or in HANDOFF.md.
  `get_page_text` and screenshots are fine for looking (password inputs render masked), but never paste their raw
  text.
- Ask in chat and wait for a clear yes before Save (Step 10), Delete (Step 16) and the cleanup question (Step 18).
  The Step 1 OK covers everything else listed in Step 1. Never push.

**Defects.** When an "Expected" line does not match: stop at that step and leave `serve` running. Capture (a) the
step number, (b) the expected line, (c) the observed value from the checker or a J-snippet (never raw page text),
(d) the last 40 log lines through the Step 5 filter. If the checker's `leaks` reports `secret_hits` above 0
anywhere, tell the owner at once which source it was (the scratch directory is deleted in Step 23 either way).
Then continue with the next step when it does not depend on the failed one; otherwise jump to Step 17. Do not edit
code in this task. Each defect becomes one bullet in the HANDOFF block (Step 19) with the suspected `path:line`; the
orchestrator turns it into a fix task (systematic debugging, a failing offline test, the fix, the gate).
Known risk, recorded ahead of time and verified on master with fake credentials and ffmpeg n9.0.2: a failing
ingest's stderr contains the line `Error opening input file rtsp://<user>:<password>@127.0.0.1:1/videoMain.`, and
`/status.json` (no login required) returns the raw stderr tail through `cli._proc_status`
(`src/rtsp_warden/cli.py:236-242` on master; not an RW-2 file). Step 13 shows whether it leaks.

    mode 0600; `allocate_proxy_port` returns 9001 when it is free.
  - Task 7: `GET /cameras/new`, `POST /cameras/new/test` (form `stream` = `main` | `sub`), `POST /cameras/new/onvif`,

    `main_url`, `sub_url`, `record_enabled` (ids equal names); buttons "Find stream URLs (ONVIF)", "Test main
    stream", "Test sub stream", "Save camera". A stream URL carrying `user:pass@` is rejected; `_probe_url` builds
    the probe URL from the User name / Password fields. An ONVIF port in the form limits the lookup to that port.
    Fragments: `Found ONVIF on port <n>.` or `ONVIF lookup failed (ports tried: ...): <reason>`; probe result
    `Codec: h264`, `Resolution: 1280x720`, `Frame rate: 15 fps` plus a `data:image/jpeg;base64,` image, or
    `Could not connect to the main stream (<redacted URL>)`. Save flash: `Camera <name> added and started.`
  - Task 8: `GET`/`POST /cameras/{name}/edit`, `POST /cameras/{name}/delete`.

**Browser snippets.** Run with `mcp__Claude_Browser__javascript_tool` (`action: "javascript_exec"`). They only
read the page; none of them returns a password or URL userinfo. Steps refer to them by name.

J1, arm the request timer (records the next htmx request to `/cameras/new/*`):
```js
window.__t12 = { start: performance.now(), ms: null, path: null, status: null };
if (!window.__t12_listener) {
  window.__t12_listener = (e) => {
    const p = e.detail.pathInfo ? e.detail.pathInfo.requestPath : "";
    if (window.__t12 && window.__t12.ms === null && p.startsWith("/cameras/new/")) {
      window.__t12.ms = Math.round(performance.now() - window.__t12.start);
      window.__t12.path = p;
      window.__t12.status = e.detail.xhr ? e.detail.xhr.status : null;
    }
  };
  document.body.addEventListener("htmx:afterRequest", window.__t12_listener);
}
"armed";
```

J2, form fields (URL userinfo masked; credentials reported as filled or not):
```js
(() => {
  const el = (n) => document.querySelector(`[name="${n}"]`);
  const val = (n) => { const e = el(n); return e ? (e.type === "checkbox" ? e.checked : e.value) : null; };
  const kind = (u) => {
    const m = (u || "").match(/^rtsps?:\/\/([^@\/]*)@/);
    if (!m) return "none";
    return /^\$\{CAM_[A-Z0-9_]+_USER\}:\$\{CAM_[A-Z0-9_]+_PASS\}$/.test(m[1]) ? "env-ref" : "literal";
  };
  const mask = (u) => (u == null ? u : u.replace(/^(rtsps?:\/\/)[^@\/]*@/, "$1<userinfo>@"));
  return {
    path: location.pathname,
    name: val("name"),
    host: val("host"),
    main_url: mask(val("main_url")),
    main_userinfo: kind(val("main_url")),
    sub_url: mask(val("sub_url")),
    sub_userinfo: kind(val("sub_url")),
    onvif_port: val("onvif_port"),
    record_enabled: val("record_enabled"),
    username_filled: !!val("username"),
    password_filled: !!val("password"),
    t12: window.__t12 || null,
  };
})()
```

J3, masked page text and a count of URL userinfo that is neither `***:***` nor `${CAM_*}` references:
```js
(() => {
  const t = document.body.innerText;
  const found = t.match(/rtsps?:\/\/[^\s@\/]*@/g) || [];
  const ok = /^rtsps?:\/\/(\*\*\*:\*\*\*|\$\{CAM_[A-Z0-9_]+_USER\}:\$\{CAM_[A-Z0-9_]+_PASS\})@$/;
  return {
    unredacted_userinfo: found.filter((x) => !ok.test(x)).length,
    includes_u_p: t.includes("u:p@"),
    text: t.replace(/(rtsps?:\/\/)[^\s@\/]*@/g, "$1<userinfo>@").slice(0, 3000),
  };
})()
```

J4, connection-test result:
```js
(() => {
  const t = document.body.innerText;
  const img = document.querySelector('img[src^="data:image/jpeg;base64,"]');
  return {
    snapshot: !!img,
    snapshot_px: img ? [img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight] : null,
    h264: /h\.?264/i.test(t),
    res_1280x720: /1280\s*[x×]\s*720/.test(t),
    fps_15: /(^|[^\d.])15(\.0+)?\s*fps/i.test(t) || /fps[^\d]{0,3}15(\.0+)?([^\d]|$)/i.test(t),
    fps_text: (t.match(/\d+(\.\d+)?\s*fps|fps\W{0,3}\d+(\.\d+)?/i) || [null])[0],
    t12: window.__t12 || null,
  };
})()
```

J5, status badges, flash messages and navigation timing on the current page:
```js
(() => {
  const nav = performance.getEntriesByType("navigation")[0];
  return {
    path: location.pathname,
    running: document.querySelectorAll(".status-running").length,
    restarting: document.querySelectorAll(".status-restarting").length,
    failed: document.querySelectorAll(".status-failed").length,
    idle: document.querySelectorAll(".status-idle").length,
    status_row_poll: !!document.querySelector('[hx-get="/cameras/foscam-c1/status-row"]'),
    flash: Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(".flash")).map(
      (e) => e.className + ": " + e.innerText.trim().replace(/(rtsps?:\/\/)[^\s@\/]*@/g, "$1<userinfo>@")
    ),
    nav: nav ? { type: nav.type, redirects: nav.redirectCount, ms: Math.round(nav.responseEnd - nav.startTime) } : null,
  };
})()
```

form submit carries the form's hidden `csrf_token`; an htmx request gets the CSRF header from `warden.js`:
```js
(() => {
  const f = document.querySelector('form[action="/cameras/foscam-c1/delete"]');
  if (f) {
    HTMLFormElement.prototype.submit.call(f);
    return "submitted form";
  }
  const b = document.querySelector('[hx-post="/cameras/foscam-c1/delete"]');
  if (b && window.htmx) {
    window.htmx.ajax("POST", "/cameras/foscam-c1/delete", { source: b });
    return "sent htmx request";
  }
  return "no delete control";
})()
```

```bash
uv run --project /home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2 python -c "import secrets; print('WARDEN_ADMIN_USERNAME=admin'); print('WARDEN_ADMIN_PASSWORD=' + secrets.token_urlsafe(18))" > /home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw2-foscam-scratch/admin.env && chmod 600 /home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw2-foscam-scratch/admin.env
WARDEN_DB_URL=sqlite:////home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw2-foscam-scratch/warden.db uv run --directory /home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw2-foscam-scratch --project /home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2 python -c "from rtsp_warden.db.schema import ensure_schema; ensure_schema(); print('schema ready')"
uv run --directory /home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw2-foscam-scratch --project /home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw-2 rtsp-warden doctor -c /home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw2-foscam-scratch/config.yaml
```
Expected: the schema command prints Alembic `INFO` lines ending with
`Running upgrade 0001_initial -> 0002_clips, add clips table`, then `schema ready`; `doctor` prints a table whose
rows read `config loads` / `OK`, `ffmpeg` / `OK (/usr/bin/ffmpeg)` and `mediamtx` / `N/A (no rtsp proxies)` (an
`ffprobe` row, if present, says OK).

Why these choices: `WARDEN_DB_URL` with four slashes is an absolute SQLite path, so the owner's real database under
`$XDG_DATA_HOME` is never touched. The schema is created in a throwaway process because on an empty database
`ensure_schema()` runs Alembic, and `migrations/env.py:30` calls `logging.config.fileConfig`, which disables every
logger created before it; `serve` then logs almost nothing, and the secret scan needs its logs (RW-3 removes that
call; this branch does not have the fix). `uv run --directory <scratch>` makes the scratch directory the working
directory, so the relative defaults `./recordings` (`config.py:108`) and `./workspace` (`config.py:505`) land
there instead of in the worktree, and `./.env` is the scratch `.env`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the checker and smoke-run it**

Create `/home/jcharles/Projects/python/rtsp-warden_v0.2.0/.worktrees/rw2-foscam-scratch/check_rw2_foscam.py` with
exactly this content (it was run against master with fake values while this plan was written: login, page scan,
log scan, wait states, segment facts and the process count all behaved as described):
```python
"""RW-2 Task 12 checker: prints facts about the scratch run and never prints a secret.

Run with the worktree's environment from any directory:
    uv run --project <WT> python <S>/check_rw2_foscam.py <command> [args]

Commands:
    config                     config.yaml entry and .env facts
    status                     /status.json summary for the camera
    wait STATE [sec]           poll /status.json until STATE (exit 1 on timeout); STATE is
                               running | down | absent | stopped (server not answering)
    segments [root]            .ts facts under <root>/foscam-c1/main (default <S>/recordings)
    segments-stable [sec] [root]
                               segment facts, wait <sec> (default 20), facts again, growth
    procs                      count ffmpeg/ffprobe processes that reference the camera
    leaks                      secret scan: serve logs, config.yaml, JSON endpoints, HTML pages
    scan <file>                secret scan of one file
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import html
import json
import os
import re
import stat
import sys
import time
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import unquote

import httpx
import yaml

from rtsp_warden.cli import _parse_dotenv_value

S = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
CAM = "foscam-c1"
SLUG = "FOSCAM_C1"
BASE = "http://127.0.0.1:8098"
USER_KEY = f"CAM_{SLUG}_USER"
PASS_KEY = f"CAM_{SLUG}_PASS"
ENV_REF_USERINFO = "${" + USER_KEY + "}:${" + PASS_KEY + "}"
PAGES = (
    "/",
    "/cameras",
    f"/cameras/{CAM}",
    f"/cameras/{CAM}/edit",
    f"/cameras/{CAM}/status-row",
    "/health",
    "/health/partial",
    "/onvif",
    "/status.json",
    "/metrics",
)
USERINFO_RE = re.compile(r"rtsps?://([^\s/@\"'<>]+)@")


def read_env_file(path: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    out: dict[str, str] = {}
    if not path.exists():
        return out
    for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        stripped = line.strip()
        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#") or "=" not in stripped:
            continue
        key, _, value = stripped.partition("=")
        out[key.strip()] = _parse_dotenv_value(value)
    return out


def read_env() -> dict[str, str]:
    """admin.env (the test login from Step 3) overlaid by .env (written by the app)."""
    return {**read_env_file(S / "admin.env"), **read_env_file(S / ".env")}


def squash(text: str) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"\s+", "", text)


def secret_needles(env: dict[str, str]) -> list[str]:
    user = env.get(USER_KEY, "")
    pw = env.get(PASS_KEY, "")
    if not pw:
        return []
    candidates = {f"{user}:{pw}@", f"{unquote(user)}:{unquote(pw)}@"}
    for value in (pw, unquote(pw), html.escape(unquote(pw))):
        if len(value) >= 6:
            candidates.add(value)
    return sorted({squash(c) for c in candidates if squash(c)})


def count_hits(text: str, needles: list[str]) -> int:
    flat = squash(text)
    return sum(flat.count(n) for n in needles)


def unredacted_userinfo(text: str) -> int:
    allowed = {"***:***", ENV_REF_USERINFO}
    return sum(1 for m in USERINFO_RE.finditer(text) if m.group(1) not in allowed)


def safe(value: object, needles: list[str]) -> object:
    text = str(value)
    return "<masked: contains a secret>" if needles and count_hits(text, needles) else value


def cmd_config() -> int:
    env = read_env()
    needles = secret_needles(env)
    cfg_path = S / "config.yaml"
    cfg_text = cfg_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    raw = yaml.safe_load(cfg_text) or {}
    cams = [c for c in (raw.get("cameras") or []) if isinstance(c, dict)]
    print("camera_names:", [safe(c.get("name"), needles) for c in cams])
    print("runtime_section_kept:", (raw.get("runtime") or {}).get("ffmpeg_loglevel") == "warning")
    entry = next((c for c in cams if c.get("name") == CAM), None)
    if entry is not None:
        url = str(entry.get("main_url", ""))
        scheme, _, rest = url.partition("://")
        netloc, _, path = rest.partition("/")
        userinfo, _, hostport = netloc.rpartition("@")
        print("main_url_scheme:", scheme)
        print("main_url_userinfo_is_env_ref:", userinfo == ENV_REF_USERINFO)
        print("main_url_hostport:", safe(hostport, needles))
        print("main_url_path:", safe("/" + path, needles))
        print("sub_url_key_present:", "sub_url" in entry)
        print("onvif_port:", entry.get("onvif_port"))
        print("record_enabled:", (entry.get("record") or {}).get("enabled"))
        proxy = entry.get("proxy") or {}
        keys = ("enabled", "mode", "stream", "port", "bind_host")
        print("proxy:", {k: proxy.get(k) for k in keys})
    env_path = S / ".env"
    print("env_exists:", env_path.exists())
    if env_path.exists():
        print("env_mode:", oct(stat.S_IMODE(env_path.stat().st_mode)))
    print("env_has_user_key:", USER_KEY in env)
    print("env_has_pass_key:", PASS_KEY in env)
    print("config_secret_hits:", count_hits(cfg_text, needles))
    print("config_unredacted_userinfo:", unredacted_userinfo(cfg_text))
    return 0


def fetch_status() -> dict:
    with urllib.request.urlopen(f"{BASE}/status.json", timeout=5) as resp:
        return json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))


def summarize(data: dict) -> dict:
    now = float(data.get("now") or time.time())
    cam = next((c for c in data.get("cameras", []) if c.get("name") == CAM), None)
    if cam is None:
        return {"present": False, "cameras": len(data.get("cameras", []))}
    streams = cam.get("streams") or {}
    main = streams.get("main") or {}
    ingest = main.get("ingest") or {}

    def age(key: str) -> float | None:
        value = main.get(key)
        return None if value is None else round(now - float(value), 1)

    url = str(main.get("source_url", ""))
    return {
        "present": True,
        "ok": cam.get("ok"),
        "streams": sorted(streams),
        "main_running": ingest.get("running"),
        "record_enabled": main.get("record_enabled"),
        "mjpeg_enabled": main.get("mjpeg_enabled"),
        "frame_age_s": age("last_frame_at"),
        "segment_age_s": age("last_segment_at"),
        "source_url_redacted": "@" not in url or "***" in url,
    }


def cmd_status() -> int:
    print("status:", summarize(fetch_status()))
    return 0


def reached(state: str, s: dict) -> bool:
    if state == "running":
        age = s.get("frame_age_s")
        return s.get("main_running") is True and age is not None and age < 5.0
    if state == "down":
        return s.get("present") is True and s.get("main_running") is not True
    if state == "absent":
        return s.get("present") is False
    if state == "stopped":
        return False
    raise SystemExit(f"unknown state {state!r}; use running, down, absent or stopped")


def cmd_wait(state: str, timeout_s: float) -> int:
    start = time.monotonic()
    last: dict = {}
    done = False
    while True:
        try:
            last = summarize(fetch_status())
            done = reached(state, last)
        except OSError as exc:
            last = {"error": type(exc).__name__}
            done = state == "stopped"
        if done or time.monotonic() - start >= timeout_s:
            break
        time.sleep(1.0)
    verdict = "reached" if done else "TIMEOUT"
    print(f"wait {state}: {verdict} after {time.monotonic() - start:.1f} s")
    print("status:", last)
    return 0 if done else 1


def seg_facts(root: Path) -> dict:
    d = root / CAM / "main"
    files = sorted(d.glob("*.ts"), key=lambda p: p.stat().st_mtime) if d.is_dir() else []
    newest = files[-1] if files else None
    return {
        "dir": str(d),
        "dir_exists": d.is_dir(),
        "count": len(files),
        "total_bytes": sum(p.stat().st_size for p in files),
        "newest_age_s": round(time.time() - newest.stat().st_mtime, 1) if newest else None,
    }


def cmd_segments(root: Path) -> int:
    print("segments:", seg_facts(root))
    return 0


def cmd_segments_stable(seconds: float, root: Path) -> int:
    before = seg_facts(root)
    time.sleep(seconds)
    after = seg_facts(root)
    print("before:", before)
    print("after: ", after)
    grew = after["total_bytes"] > before["total_bytes"] or after["count"] > before["count"]
    print(f"grew_in_{seconds:g}s:", grew)
    return 0


def cmd_procs() -> int:
    me = os.getpid()
    pids: list[int] = []
    for entry in Path("/proc").iterdir():
        if not entry.name.isdigit() or int(entry.name) == me:
            continue
        try:
            argv = (entry / "cmdline").read_bytes().split(b"\0")
        except OSError:
            continue
        if not argv or not argv[0]:
            continue
        exe = os.path.basename(argv[0].decode(errors="replace"))
        if not exe.startswith(("ffmpeg", "ffprobe")):
            continue
        if any(b"videoMain" in a or b"192.168.1.72" in a for a in argv[1:]):
            pids.append(int(entry.name))
    print("camera_ffmpeg_processes:", len(pids), "pids:", sorted(pids))
    return 0


def password_inputs_empty(page: str) -> bool | None:
    tags = re.findall(r"<input\b[^>]*\bname=[\"']password[\"'][^>]*>", page, flags=re.I)
    if not tags:
        return None
    return all(not re.search(r"\bvalue=[\"'][^\"']+[\"']", t, flags=re.I) for t in tags)


def report(name: str, text: str, needles: list[str]) -> bool:
    hits = count_hits(text, needles)
    raw = unredacted_userinfo(text)
    print(f"{name}: secret_hits={hits} unredacted_userinfo={raw}")
    return hits == 0 and raw == 0


def cmd_leaks() -> int:
    env = read_env()
    needles = secret_needles(env)
    if not needles:
        print(f"note: {PASS_KEY} not in .env; only the unredacted-userinfo check runs")
    clean = True
    for log in sorted(S.glob("serve-*.log")):
        clean &= report(log.name, log.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"), needles)
    clean &= report("config.yaml", (S / "config.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"), needles)
    with httpx.Client(base_url=BASE, timeout=15.0, follow_redirects=False) as client:
        try:
            client.get("/login", headers={"Accept": "text/html"})
        except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
            print(f"server not reachable ({type(exc).__name__}); pages skipped")
            print("LEAK CHECK:", "PASS (files only)" if clean else "FAIL")
            return 0 if clean else 1
        token = client.cookies.get("warden_csrf") or ""
        resp = client.post(
            "/login",
            data={
                "username": env.get("WARDEN_ADMIN_USERNAME", "admin"),
                "password": env.get("WARDEN_ADMIN_PASSWORD", ""),
                "csrf_token": token,
                "next": "/",
            },
        )
        logged_in = resp.status_code == 303 and client.cookies.get("warden_session") is not None
        print("login:", "ok" if logged_in else f"FAILED (status {resp.status_code})")
        for path in PAGES:
            page = client.get(path, headers={"Accept": "text/html"})
            clean &= report(f"GET {path} [{page.status_code}]", page.text, needles)
            if path.endswith("/edit") and page.status_code == 200:
                print("edit_password_inputs_empty:", password_inputs_empty(page.text))
    print("LEAK CHECK:", "PASS" if clean else "FAIL")
    return 0 if clean else 1


def cmd_scan(path: Path) -> int:
    needles = secret_needles(read_env())
    ok = report(str(path), path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"), needles)
    print("SCAN:", "PASS" if ok else "FAIL")
    return 0 if ok else 1


def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
    if not argv:
        print(__doc__)
        return 2
    cmd, rest = argv[0], argv[1:]
    default_root = S / "recordings"
    if cmd == "config":
        return cmd_config()
    if cmd == "status":
        return cmd_status()
    if cmd == "wait":
        return cmd_wait(rest[0], float(rest[1]) if len(rest) > 1 else 45.0)
    if cmd == "segments":
        return cmd_segments(Path(rest[0]) if rest else default_root)
    if cmd == "segments-stable":
        seconds = float(rest[0]) if rest else 20.0
        return cmd_segments_stable(seconds, Path(rest[1]) if len(rest) > 1 else default_root)
    if cmd == "procs":
        return cmd_procs()
    if cmd == "leaks":
        return cmd_leaks()
    if cmd == "scan":
        return cmd_scan(Path(rest[0]))
    print(__doc__)
    return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))
```

answering), `form_input` ONVIF port = `888` (with a port set, only that port is tried), run J1 and click "Find
stream URLs (ONVIF)" once more. If it still fails, record the defect, then `form_input` by hand: ONVIF port =
`888`, Main stream = `rtsp://192.168.1.72:554/videoMain`, Sub stream empty, and continue.

   ```markdown
   ## {DATE} — RW-2 Task 12: manual Foscam check of `feat/ui-pass` at `{SHA}`: {VERDICT}

   Run with the owner's OK; not a merge gate. `serve --web-port 8098` ran from the scratch folder
   `.worktrees/rw2-foscam-scratch` (config, `.env`, database, logs, recordings; never committed; {CLEANUP}).
   {FFMPEG}. Camera: Foscam C1 V3 through 192.168.1.72 (RTSP 554, ONVIF 888), saved with `videoMain` only.

   | Step | Check | Expected | Observed |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | 7 | Test connection, dead local URL | error in < 3 s; no `u:p@` on the page or in the log | {S7} |
   | 8 | ONVIF fill | port 888; main `rtsp://192.168.1.72:554/videoMain`, sub `.../videoSub`; < 12 s | {S8} |
   | 9 | Test connection, `videoMain` | h264, 1280x720, 15 fps, 640x360 snapshot | {S9} |
   | 10 | Save + hot-add | 303 to `/cameras/foscam-c1`, success flash, < 20 s | {S10} |
   | 11 | Running | frames < 5 s old; 1 ffmpeg; `.ts` written; YAML uses `${CAM_FOSCAM_C1_*}`; no `sub_url`; `onvif_port: 888`; `.env` 0600; secret scan clean | {S11} |
   | 12 | 375 px width | no horizontal overflow on `/cameras` and the detail page | {S12} |
   | 13 | Failure display | restarting/failed with countdown and a redacted error line; secret scan clean; recovers | {S13} |
   | 14 | `record.enabled` off | restart; `record_enabled False`; no segment growth in 20 s; preview live | {S14} |
   | 15 | `serve` restart from another cwd | camera running from the config-dir `.env` | {S15} |
   | 16 | Delete | 303 to `/cameras`; gone from YAML, runtime and `/status.json`; 0 ffmpeg; recordings kept | {S16} |

   **Defects.** {DEFECTS}

   **Observations.** {OBSERVATIONS}
   ```
   Fill rules:
   - `{DATE}`: the `date +%F` output. `{SHA}`: the `rev-parse` output. `{FFMPEG}`: the first line of
     `ffmpeg -version` up to the version, e.g. `ffmpeg version n9.0.2`.
   - `{VERDICT}`: `PASS` when every row is PASS; `PASS with <n> defect(s)` when the walkthrough finished with FAIL
     rows; `stopped at step <k>` when it could not finish.
   - `{S7}` to `{S16}`: `PASS` plus the measured numbers (for example `PASS (0.4 s)` or `PASS (port 888, 3.1 s)`),
     or `FAIL: <checker line or J-snippet value>`, or `not run: <reason>`. Never raw page text, never URL userinfo.
   - `{CLEANUP}`: `deleted after the run`, `credential files deleted, recordings kept`, or
